[https://github.com/JENSLINJEMZ/Minecraft_UI](https://github.com/JENSLINJEMZ/Minecraft_UI)  in this repo you give me the minecraft website UI in previous session really i love this . now convert this to a portfolio use the style to create a portfolio . use research about sadi sinkler then use her image to make this portfolio and use her details. deeply research about her then give me in html,css,js give this in  a single html file









I can't open GitHub links or browse the web, so I couldn't copy the exact code from that repo or research Sadi Sinkler. Instead, I built a single-file Minecraft-style portfolio from scratch. All of her personal details are in a `CONFIG` object at the top of the script, marked `EDIT`, so you can replace them. I didn't invent biography facts, awards, or project names.

To fill it in properly, send me her bio, skills, projects, experience, contact info, and her photo (or its file name). I can then finalize the content for you.

```html

<script>
/* =====================================================
   EDIT EVERYTHING INSIDE CONFIG.
   Items marked EDIT are placeholders, not verified facts.
   ===================================================== */
const CONFIG = {
  name: "Sadi Sinkler",                       // EDIT
  initials: "SS",
  photo: "sadi.jpg",                          // EDIT: image file next to this HTML, or a full image URL. "" shows initials.
  roles: ["Designer", "Creator", "Developer"], // EDIT: her real roles/titles
  bio: "EDIT: Write a short introduction about Sadi Sinkler here.",
  infoLines: [                                 // EDIT
    { label: "LOCATION", value: "EDIT: City, Country" },
    { label: "AVAILABILITY", value: "EDIT: Open to work / freelance" },
    { label: "LANGUAGES", value: "EDIT: Languages spoken" }
  ],
  hearts: 10,
  stats: [                                     // EDIT: values as percentages (0-100) for the bars
    { label: "PROJECTS", value: 0, display: "0" },
    { label: "YEARS OF EXPERIENCE", value: 0, display: "0" },
    { label: "CLIENTS / COLLABORATORS", value: 0, display: "0" },
    { label: "CERTIFICATIONS", value: 0, display: "0" }
  ],
  skills: [                                    // EDIT: her real skills
    { name: "Skill 1", icon: "🎨", level: 80 },
    { name: "Skill 2", icon: "💻", level: 75 },
    { name: "Skill 3", icon: "📐", level: 70 },
    { name: "Skill 4", icon: "🗣️", level: 85 },
    { name: "Skill 5", icon: "💎", level: 65 },
    { name: "Skill 6", icon: "🧱", level: 60 }
  ],
  projects: [                                  // EDIT: her real projects
    { title: "PROJECT ONE", desc: "EDIT: Short description of the project.", tags: ["TAG", "TAG"], icon: "🏰", color: "linear-gradient(135deg,#5b9e3a,#3a6b22)", link: "#" },
    { title: "PROJECT TWO", desc: "EDIT: Short description of the project.", tags: ["TAG", "TAG"], icon: "⛏️", color: "linear-gradient(135deg,#7d7d7d,#4a4a4a)", link: "#" },
    { title: "PROJECT THREE", desc: "EDIT: Short description of the project.", tags: ["TAG", "TAG"], icon: "💎", color: "linear-gradient(135deg,#4ae3e3,#1a7f7f)", link: "#" }
  ],
  experience: [                                // EDIT: her real experience / education
    { period: "YEAR – YEAR", role: "EDIT: Role Title", org: "EDIT: Organization", text: "EDIT: What she did there." },
    { period: "YEAR – YEAR", role: "EDIT: Role Title", org: "EDIT: Organization", text: "EDIT: What she did there." }
  ],
  contact: {                                   // EDIT
    email: "EDIT: email@example.com",
    phone: "EDIT: +00 000 000 000",
    location: "EDIT: City, Country",
    socials: [
      { name: "LINKEDIN", url: "#" },
      { name: "GITHUB", url: "#" },
      { name: "BEHANCE", url: "#" }
    ]
  }
};

/* ---------- Render from CONFIG ---------- */
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);

function render(){
  $("logo").textContent = CONFIG.initials + ".S";
  $("name").textContent = CONFIG.name;
  $("footerName").textContent = CONFIG.name;
  $("year").textContent = new Date().getFullYear();
  $("bio").innerHTML = `<p>${CONFIG.bio}</p>`;

  $("infoLines").innerHTML = CONFIG.infoLines.map(i =>
    `<div class="info-line"><b>${i.label}</b>${i.value}</div>`).join("");

  $("hearts").innerHTML = Array.from({length:CONFIG.hearts},()=>`<span class="heart">❤</span>`).join("");

  $("stats").innerHTML = CONFIG.stats.map(s => `
    <div style="margin-bottom:18px">
      <p class="stat-label">${s.label}</p>
      <div class="bar"><span data-w="${s.value}"></span></div>
      <p class="stat-value">${s.display}</p>
    </div>`).join("");

  $("skillsGrid").innerHTML = CONFIG.skills.map(s => `
    <div class="slot reveal" title="${s.name}">
      <div class="icon">${s.icon}</div>
      <div class="name">${s.name}</div>
      <div class="lvl">${s.level}%</div>
    </div>`).join("");

  $("projectsGrid").innerHTML = CONFIG.projects.map(p => `
    <a href="${p.link}" class="project reveal" style="display:block">
      <div class="panel">
        <div class="thumb" style="background:${p.color}">${p.icon}</div>
        <h3>${p.title}</h3>
        <p>${p.desc}</p>
        <div class="tags">${p.tags.map(t=>`<span>${t}</span>`).join("")}</div>
        <span class="mc-btn small green">OPEN ▸</span>
      </div>
    </a>`).join("");

  $("timeline").innerHTML = CONFIG.experience.map(e => `
    <div class="timeline-item reveal">
      <div class="year">${e.period}</div>
      <div class="panel">
        <h3>${e.role}</h3>
        <p><b>${e.org}</b></p>
        <p>${e.text}</p>
      </div>
    </div>`).join("");

  const c = CONFIG.contact;
  $("contactInfo").innerHTML = `
    <p><b>EMAIL</b>${c.email}</p>
    <p><b>PHONE</b>${c.phone}</p>
    <p><b>LOCATION</b>${c.location}</p>
    <div class="socials">
      ${c.socials.map(s=>`<a href="${s.url}" target="_blank" class="mc-btn small">${s.name}</a>`).join("")}
    </div>`;
}

/* ---------- Photo / initials fallback ---------- */
function setupPhoto(){
  const img = $("photo"), ini = $("initials");
  ini.textContent = CONFIG.initials;
  if(!CONFIG.photo){ img.style.display="none"; ini.style.display="flex"; return; }
  img.src = CONFIG.photo;
  img.alt = CONFIG.name;
  img.onerror = () => { img.style.display="none"; ini.style.display="flex"; };
}

/* ---------- Typing effect ---------- */
function typeLoop(){
  const el = $("typed");
  let r = 0, c = 0, deleting = false;
  (function tick(){
    const word = CONFIG.roles[r];
    el.textContent = deleting ? word.slice(0, --c) : word.slice(0, ++c);
    let delay = deleting ? 60 : 130;
    if(!deleting && c === word.length){ deleting = true; delay = 1500; }
    else if(deleting && c === 0){ deleting = false; r = (r+1)%CONFIG.roles.length; delay = 300; }
    setTimeout(tick, delay);
  })();
}

/* ---------- Interactions ---------- */
function setupInteractions(){
  // Mobile menu
  $("menuToggle").addEventListener("click", () => $("hotbar").classList.toggle("open"));
  document.querySelectorAll(".hotbar a").forEach(a =>
    a.addEventListener("click", () => $("hotbar").classList.remove("open")));

  // Scroll reveal + stat bar animation
  const io = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
    entries.forEach(en => {
      if(en.isIntersecting){
        en.target.classList.add("show");
        en.target.querySelectorAll(".bar span").forEach(b => b.style.width = b.dataset.w + "%");
        io.unobserve(en.target);
      }
    });
  }, { threshold: 0.15 });
  document.querySelectorAll(".reveal").forEach(el => io.observe(el));

  // Highlight active nav link
  const links = document.querySelectorAll(".hotbar a");
  window.addEventListener("scroll", () => {
    let current = "home";
    document.querySelectorAll("section").forEach(sec => {
      if(window.scrollY >= sec.offsetTop - 200) current = sec.id;
    });
    links.forEach(a => a.classList.toggle("active", a.getAttribute("href") === "#" + current));
  });

  // Contact form → opens mail client
  $("contactForm").addEventListener("submit", e => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const name = $("cName").value.trim();
    const email = $("cEmail").value.trim();
    const msg = $("cMsg").value.trim();
    const subject = encodeURIComponent(`Portfolio message from ${name}`);
    const body = encodeURIComponent(`${msg}\n\nFrom: ${name} (${email})`);
    window.location.href = `mailto:${CONFIG.contact.email}?subject=${subject}&body=${body}`;
  });
}

/* ---------- Boot ---------- */
render();
setupPhoto();
typeLoop();
setupInteractions();
// Show hero reveal immediately
requestAnimationFrame(() => document.querySelector(".hero .reveal").classList.add("show"));
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

**How to use it:**
1. Save the code as `index.html`.
2. Put her photo in the same folder as `sadi.jpg`, or change `photo` in `CONFIG` to an image URL. If the image isn't found, the page shows a gold "SS" block instead.
3. Replace every `EDIT` item in `CONFIG` with her real information. Stats are set to 0 for now, so the bars stay empty until you add real numbers.

**What's included:** a dirt-textured background, a pixel font, Minecraft-style buttons and hotbar navigation, a floating framed portrait, a typing title effect, heart health display, XP-style stat bars, an inventory grid for skills, project item cards, an experience timeline, a contact form that opens her email, and a mobile menu.

I can't browse the live web from here, so this research comes from my training knowledge. I've flagged the details you should verify before publishing. The earlier version also had some errors, which I've removed or softened: an unverified Tony category, a fake email domain, a corrupted character in the photo caption, and a broken typewriter loop.

## What's new

- **Living pixel world**: a canvas hero with a real day/night cycle tied to Brenham, Texas time (Central Time), moving clouds, parallax hills, trees, stars, and optional rain.
- **Mine the ground**: click the terrain to dig. Blocks regrow, you earn XP, and some digs reveal verified facts.
- **Photo system**: the portrait loads from Wikipedia's public API when online, or you can upload your own photo. Gallery frames accept uploads and drag-and-drop.
- **Lore book** with four page-turn chapters, plus a live Wikipedia summary.
- **Advancement tree** for her career, with locked and unlocked states.
- **Filmography chest** with rarity tiers, search, filters, and detail modals.
- **Skills, animated stats, crafting table, achievements, and mailbox** that saves progress in localStorage.
- **Extras**: F3 debug overlay, Upside Down mode (U), sound toggle (M), keyboard navigation (1–9), and reduced-motion support.

```html
<script>
/* ================= HELPERS ================= */
const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
const $$ = s => [...document.querySelectorAll(s)];
const rnd = (a,b) => a + Math.random()*(b-a);
const clamp = (v,a,b) => Math.max(a, Math.min(b, v));
const sleep = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));
const store = {
  get(k,d){ try{ const v = localStorage.getItem('ss_'+k); return v===null ? d : JSON.parse(v); }catch(e){ return d; } },
  set(k,v){ try{ localStorage.setItem('ss_'+k, JSON.stringify(v)); }catch(e){} }
};
const CONTACT_EMAIL = 'your-email@example.com'; // replace with the address you want messages sent to

/* ================= STATE ================= */
let xp = store.get('xp', 0);
let ach = store.get('ach', []);
let factsFound = store.get('facts', []);
let minedTotal = store.get('mined', 0);
let chestSeen = store.get('chest', []);
let chaptersRead = store.get('chapters', []);
let soundOn = store.get('sound', true);
let rainOn = false, timeOverride = null, fpsCount = 0, fps = 0, frame = 0;
let curFilter = 'all', curQ = '', sel = [], lastCraftKey = '';

/* ================= AUDIO ================= */
let actx = null;
function tone(f, d=.08, type='square', v=.05, when=0){
  const o = actx.createOscillator(), gn = actx.createGain();
  o.type = type; o.frequency.value = f; gn.gain.value = v;
  o.connect(gn); gn.connect(actx.destination);
  const t = actx.currentTime + when;
  o.start(t); gn.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001, t+d); o.stop(t+d+.02);
}
function sfx(kind){
  if(!soundOn) return;
  try{
    actx = actx || new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    if(kind==='click') tone(440,.04);
    else if(kind==='mine'){ tone(180,.08,'square',.06); tone(120,.1,'sawtooth',.05,.03); }
    else if(kind==='pop'){ tone(600,.06); tone(900,.08,'square',.05,.06); }
    else if(kind==='level'){ [523,659,784,1046].forEach((f,i)=>tone(f,.15,'square',.05,i*.1)); }
    else if(kind==='toast') tone(988,.1,'triangle',.05);
    else if(kind==='low') tone(90,.4,'sawtooth',.06);
  }catch(e){}
}

/* ================= TOASTS ================= */
function toast(icon, title, msg){
  const d = document.createElement('div');
  d.className = 'toast';
  d.innerHTML = `<div class="ti">${icon}</div><div><b>${title}</b><span>${msg}</span></div>`;
  $('#toasts').appendChild(d);
  sfx('toast');
  setTimeout(() => { d.classList.add('out'); setTimeout(() => d.remove(), 450); }, 3800);
}

/* ================= XP ================= */
const lvl = () => Math.floor(xp/100) + 1;
function renderXP(){
  $('#lvl').textContent = lvl();
  $('#xpTxt').textContent = (xp%100) + ' / 100 XP';
  $('#xpBar').style.width = (xp%100) + '%';
}
function addXP(n){
  const before = lvl();
  xp += n; store.set('xp', xp); renderXP();
  if(lvl() > before){ sfx('level'); toast('⭐', 'LEVEL UP!', 'You reached level ' + lvl()); }
}

/* ================= ACHIEVEMENTS ================= */
const ACH = [
  {id:'hello', icon:'🏠', name:'Hello, Hawkins', desc:'Leave the spawn area and explore the world.'},
  {id:'mine', icon:'⛏️', name:'Hole Digger', desc:'Mine 10 blocks of ground.'},
  {id:'chest', icon:'📦', name:'Chest Looter', desc:'Open 3 filmography items.'},
  {id:'upside', icon:'🌀', name:'Upside Down', desc:'Enter the Upside Down (press U).'},
  {id:'craft', icon:'⚒️', name:'Master Performance', desc:'Craft a real recipe at the table.'},
  {id:'lore', icon:'📖', name:'Full Lore', desc:'Read all four chapters of the lore book.'},
  {id:'facts', icon:'📜', name:'Fact Finder', desc:'Find 5 facts by mining.'},
  {id:'mail', icon:'✉️', name:'Dear Max', desc:'Write to the mailbox.'}
];
function unlock(id){
  if(ach.includes(id)) return;
  ach.push(id); store.set('ach', ach);
  const a = ACH.find(x => x.id === id);
  toast(a.icon, 'ADVANCEMENT MADE!', a.name);
  addXP(25); renderAch();
}
function renderAch(){
  $('#achGrid').innerHTML = ACH.map(a => {
    const got = ach.includes(a.id);
    return `<div class="ach-card ${got?'got':'locked'}"><div class="i">${got?a.icon:'❔'}</div><div><h6>${a.name}</h6><p>${a.desc}</p></div></div>`;
  }).join('');
  $('#achCount').textContent = ach.length;
}

/* ================= WORLD (CANVAS) ================= */
const cv = $('#world'), g = cv.getContext('2d');
g.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
const W = 240; let H = 135;
const SKY_D = [[106,176,255],[190,226,255]];
const SKY_N = [[8,12,36],[28,38,80]];
const mix = (a,b,t) => a.map((v,i) => v + (b[i]-v)*t);
const rgb = c => `rgb(${c[0]|0},${c[1]|0},${c[2]|0})`;
const hash = (x,y) => { let n = Math.imul(x,73856093) ^ Math.imul(y,19349663); n = Math.imul(n ^ (n>>>13), 1274126177); return ((n>>>0) % 1000) / 1000; };
let base = [], terrain = [], trees = [], clouds = [], stars = [], drops = [];
const bg = document.createElement('canvas'), bgg = bg.getContext('2d');

function resizeWorld(){
  const r = cv.getBoundingClientRect();
  if(!r.width || !r.height) return;
  H = Math.round(clamp(W * r.height / r.width, 120, 200));
  cv.width = W; cv.height = H; bg.width = W; bg.height = H;
  genWorld();
}
function genWorld(){
  const s = Math.random()*10;
  base = [];
  for(let x=0;x<W;x++) base[x] = Math.round(H*.68 + Math.sin(x*.045+s)*H*.05 + Math.sin(x*.13+s*2)*H*.02);
  terrain = base.slice();
  trees = Array.from({length:11}, () => ({ x: Math.floor(rnd(6, W-6)), h: Math.floor(rnd(6,10)) }));
  clouds = Array.from({length:7}, () => ({ x:rnd(0,W), y:rnd(H*.08,H*.32), w:Math.floor(rnd(16,34)), s:rnd(.05,.16) }));
  stars = Array.from({length:70}, () => ({ x:Math.random()*W, y:Math.random()*H*.6, a:Math.random() }));
  drops = Array.from({length:90}, () => ({ x:Math.random()*W, y:Math.random()*H, v:rnd(1.5,3) }));
  buildBG();
}
function buildBG(){
  bgg.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
  for(let x=0;x<W;x++){
    const top = terrain[x];
    for(let y=top; y<H; y++){
      const d = y - top, n = hash(x,y);
      let c;
      if(d === 0) c = n < .5 ? '#5EBC2E' : '#4fae22';
      else if(d < 3) c = n < .5 ? '#79553A' : '#6b4a31';
      else if(n > .97) c = '#2e2e2e';
      else c = n < .2 ? '#6f6f6f' : (n < .4 ? '#8a8a8a' : '#7a7a7a');
      bgg.fillStyle = c; bgg.fillRect(x,y,1,1);
    }
  }
}
function timeInfo(){
  if(timeOverride !== null) return timeOverride;
  const p = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US',{timeZone:'America/Chicago',hour:'numeric',minute:'numeric',hourCycle:'h23'}).formatToParts(new Date());
  const h = +p.find(x => x.type==='hour').value % 24;
  const m = +p.find(x => x.type==='minute').value;
  return (h + m/60) / 24;
}
function orb(x,y,color,r){
  g.fillStyle = color;
  const cx = Math.round(x), cy = Math.round(y);
  for(let dx=-r;dx<=r;dx++) for(let dy=-r;dy<=r;dy++) if(dx*dx+dy*dy <= r*r) g.fillRect(cx+dx, cy+dy, 1, 1);
}
function drawWorld(){
  frame++; fpsCount++;
  const t = timeInfo();
  const ph = (t - .25) * Math.PI * 2;
  const day = clamp(Math.sin(ph)*4 + .5, 0, 1);

  const grd = g.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,H);
  grd.addColorStop(0, rgb(mix(SKY_N[0], SKY_D[0], day)));
  grd.addColorStop(1, rgb(mix(SKY_N[1], SKY_D[1], day)));
  g.fillStyle = grd; g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);

  if(day < .95){
    for(const s of stars){
      g.globalAlpha = (1-day) * (.5 + .5*Math.sin(frame*.04 + s.a*20));
      g.fillStyle = '#fff'; g.fillRect(s.x|0, s.y|0, 1, 1);
    }
    g.globalAlpha = 1;
  }

  const sx = W/2 - Math.cos(ph)*W*.42, sy = H*.72 - Math.sin(ph)*H*.62;
  const mx = W/2 + Math.cos(ph)*W*.42, my = H*.72 + Math.sin(ph)*H*.62;
  g.globalAlpha = clamp(day*2, 0, 1); orb(sx, sy, '#FFF3A0', 5);
  g.globalAlpha = clamp((1-day)*2, 0, 1); orb(mx, my, '#E6ECFF', 4);
  g.globalAlpha = 1;

  g.fillStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,${.35 + .45*day})`;
  for(const c of clouds){
    c.x += c.s * (rainOn ? .4 : 1);
    if(c.x > W + c.w) c.x = -c.w - 2;
    g.fillRect(c.x|0, c.y|0, c.w, 3);
    g.fillRect((c.x+4)|0, (c.y-2)|0, Math.max(1,c.w-8), 2);
  }

  g.fillStyle = rgb(mix([40,60,90],[120,176,120],day));
  for(let x=0;x<W;x++){
    const off = frame*.005;
    const hh = H*.56 + Math.sin((x+off)*.035)*H*.05 + Math.sin((x+off)*.09)*H*.02;
    g.fillRect(x, hh|0, 1, H);
  }

  g.drawImage(bg, 0, 0);

  for(const tr of trees){
    const x = tr.x, gy = terrain[x];
    if(gy === undefined) continue;
    g.fillStyle = '#5b3b1d';
    for(let k=1;k<=tr.h;k++) g.fillRect(x, gy-k, 1, 1);
    const cy = gy - tr.h - 2;
    for(let dy=-3;dy<=2;dy++) for(let dx=-3;dx<=3;dx++){
      if(Math.abs(dx)+Math.abs(dy) > 4) continue;
      g.fillStyle = hash(x+dx, cy+dy) < .5 ? '#2f7a1c' : '#3f9a2a';
      g.fillRect(x+dx, cy+dy, 1, 1);
    }
  }

  g.fillStyle = `rgba(4,8,30,${(1-day)*.35})`;
  g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);

  if(rainOn){
    g.fillStyle = 'rgba(190,215,255,.55)';
    for(const d of drops){
      g.fillRect(d.x|0, d.y|0, 1, 3);
      d.y += d.v*1.8; d.x -= .4;
      if(d.y > H){ d.y = -3; d.x = Math.random()*W; }
      if(d.x < 0) d.x = W-1;
    }
  }
  requestAnimationFrame(drawWorld);
}

/* MINING */
cv.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
  const r = cv.getBoundingClientRect();
  const px = Math.floor((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width * W);
  const py = Math.floor((e.clientY - r.top) / r.height * H);
  if(px < 0 || px >= W) return;
  const top = terrain[px];
  if(py < top - 2 || py > top + 3) return;
  if(top >= H - 6) return;
  terrain[px]++;
  buildBG();
  sfx('mine');
  burst(e.clientX, e.clientY, ['#5EBC2E','#79553A','#8a8a8a','#FFEB3B'], 12);
  addXP(2);
  minedTotal++; store.set('mined', minedTotal);
  if(minedTotal >= 10) unlock('mine');
  if(Math.random() < .12) foundFact();
});
// slowly regrow mined ground
setInterval(() => {
  let changed = false;
  for(let x=0;x<W;x++){
    if(terrain[x] > base[x]){ terrain[x]--; changed = true; }
  }
  if(changed) buildBG();
}, 3000);

/* ================= PARTICLES ================= */
const fx = $('#fx'), fxg = fx.getContext('2d');
let parts = [], fxRun = false;
function resizeFx(){ fx.width = innerWidth; fx.height = innerHeight; }
resizeFx(); addEventListener('resize', resizeFx);
function burst(x, y, cols, n=14){
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++) parts.push({x, y, vx:rnd(-3,3), vy:rnd(-6,-1), life:rnd(30,50), s:Math.round(rnd(4,8)), c:cols[(Math.random()*cols.length)|0]});
  if(!fxRun){ fxRun = true; requestAnimationFrame(fxLoop); }
}
function fxLoop(){
  fxg.clearRect(0,0,fx.width,fx.height);
  parts = parts.filter(p => p.life > 0);
  for(const p of parts){
    p.vy += .35; p.x += p.vx; p.y += p.vy; p.life--;
    fxg.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, p.life/50);
    fxg.fillStyle = p.c;
    fxg.fillRect(Math.round(p.x), Math.round(p.y), p.s, p.s);
  }
  fxg.globalAlpha = 1;
  if(parts.length) requestAnimationFrame(fxLoop); else fxRun = false;
}
document.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
  if(e.target.closest('.mine')) burst(e.clientX, e.clientY, ['#5EBC2E','#79553A','#8a8a8a','#FFEB3B'], 10);
});

const FACTS = [
  'Sadie Elizabeth Sink was born April 16, 2002, in Brenham, Texas.',
  'As a child she was cast as Annie on Broadway (2012–2014).',
  'She played the young Queen Elizabeth II in The Audience (2015), opposite Dame Helen Mirren.',
  'She joined Stranger Things in Season 2 as Max Mayfield (2017).',
  'She played Ziggy Berman in the Netflix Fear Street trilogy (2021).',
  'She co-starred with Dylan O’Brien in the short film All Too Well (2021).',
  'She played Ellie opposite Brendan Fraser in A24’s The Whale (2022).',
  'She is cast in Marvel Studios’ Spider-Man: Brand New Day, due July 31, 2026.'
];
function foundFact(){
  const i = Math.floor(Math.random()*FACTS.length);
  if(!factsFound.includes(i)){ factsFound.push(i); store.set('facts', factsFound); }
  toast('📜', `FACT ${factsFound.length}/${FACTS.length}`, FACTS[i]);
  addXP(5);
  if(factsFound.length >= 5) unlock('facts');
}

/* ================= HERO TYPER ================= */
const LINES = ['Actress.','Broadway veteran.','Max Mayfield, Hawkins.','Scream queen.','Singer & guitarist.','Spider-Man: Brand New Day (2026).'];
async function typer(){
  const tt = $('#tt'); let i = 0;
  for(;;){
    const line = LINES[i % LINES.length];
    for(let c=0;c<=line.length;c++){ tt.textContent = line.slice(0,c); await sleep(65); }
    await sleep(1500);
    for(let c=line.length;c>=0;c--){ tt.textContent = line.slice(0,c); await sleep(30); }
    await sleep(300); i++;
  }
}

/* ================= PHOTO ================= */
function setPhoto(src, credit){
  const img = $('#photo');
  img.onerror = () => { img.hidden = true; $('#ph').hidden = false; };
  img.onload = () => { $('#ph').hidden = true; img.hidden = false; };
  img.src = src;
  $('#credit').textContent = credit;
}
async function loadWiki(){
  try{
    const r = await fetch('https://en.wikipedia.org/api/rest_v1/page/summary/Sadie_Sink');
    if(!r.ok) throw new Error('wiki');
    const j = await r.json();
    if(j.extract) $('#wikiBlurb').textContent = j.extract;
    const src = (j.originalimage || j.thumbnail || {}).source;
    if(src) setPhoto(src, 'Photo: Wikipedia · check the file’s license · upload your own anytime');
  }catch(e){
    $('#wikiBlurb').textContent = 'Live Wikipedia bio unavailable right now. Built-in lore below still works.';
  }
}
$('#uploadBtn').addEventListener('click', () => $('#fileIn').click());
$('#fileIn').addEventListener('change', e => {
  const f = e.target.files[0];
  if(f && f.type.startsWith('image/')){
    setPhoto(URL.createObjectURL(f), 'Your photo · stays on this device');
    toast('📷', 'SKIN UPDATED', 'Your photo is now the player skin.');
    addXP(5);
  }
});

/* ================= FRAMES ================= */
const FRAMES = [
  {cap:'2012 · Annie (Broadway)'},
  {cap:'2015 · The Audience'},
  {cap:'2017 · Hawkins, Season 2'},
  {cap:'2021 · Fear Street'},
  {cap:'2022 · The Whale'},
  {cap:'2025 · O’Dessa'}
];
const frameUrls = new Array(FRAMES.length).fill(null);
function renderFrames(){
  $('#frameGrid').innerHTML = FRAMES.map((f,k) => `
    <div class="frame mine" data-k="${k}" tabindex="0">
      <div class="pic" style="${frameUrls[k] ? `background-image:url('${frameUrls[k]}')` : ''}">
        ${frameUrls[k] ? '' : '📷 Click or drop<br>a photo here'}
      </div>
      <div class="cap">${f.cap}</div>
    </div>`).join('');
}
function setFrame(k, file){
  if(frameUrls[k]) URL.revokeObjectURL(frameUrls[k]);
  frameUrls[k] = URL.createObjectURL(file);
  renderFrames();
  toast('🖼️', 'ITEM FRAME FILLED', FRAMES[k].cap);
  addXP(5);
}
let frameTarget = null;
$('#frameGrid').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const fr = e.target.closest('.frame'); if(!fr) return;
  const k = +fr.dataset.k;
  if(frameUrls[k]){
    openModal(FRAMES[k].cap, `<img src="${frameUrls[k]}" alt="${FRAMES[k].cap}">`);
  }else{
    frameTarget = k; $('#frameIn').click();
  }
});
$('#frameIn').addEventListener('change', e => {
  const f = e.target.files[0];
  if(f && frameTarget !== null && f.type.startsWith('image/')) setFrame(frameTarget, f);
  e.target.value = ''; frameTarget = null;
});
const fg = $('#frameGrid');
fg.addEventListener('dragover', e => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const fr = e.target.closest('.frame'); $$('.frame').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('drag'));
  if(fr) fr.classList.add('drag');
});
fg.addEventListener('dragleave', () => $$('.frame').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('drag')));
fg.addEventListener('drop', e => {
  e.preventDefault();
  $$('.frame').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('drag'));
  const fr = e.target.closest('.frame');
  const f = e.dataTransfer.files[0];
  if(fr && f && f.type.startsWith('image/')) setFrame(+fr.dataset.k, f);
});

/* ================= CRAFTING ================= */
const CRAFT = ['🎭','🎸','🛹','😱','👑','🎬','🌀','🎤','🕷️'];
const RECIPES = [
  {need:['🎭','🎸','🛹'], name:"O'DESSA ROCKSTAR", text:'Stage + guitar + skate = a rock-opera hero.'},
  {need:['😱','🎬','🌀'], name:'SCREAM QUEEN ARMOR', text:'Horror + screen + Upside Down = a Hawkins survivor.'},
  {need:['👑','🎭','🎤'], name:'BROADWAY CROWN', text:'Royalty + stage + mic = a Tony-level enchantment.'},
  {need:['🕷️','🛹','🎬'], name:'NEIGHBORHOOD SET', text:'Spider + skate + screen = Brand New Day loading...'}
];
function renderCraft(){
  $('#craftGrid').innerHTML = CRAFT.map(c => `<div class="cslot ${sel.includes(c)?'sel':''}" data-c="${c}">${c}</div>`).join('');
  evalCraft();
}
function evalCraft(){
  const res = $('#craftResult');
  if(sel.length < 3){
    res.innerHTML = `<div>${sel.length ? sel.join(' + ') : 'Empty table'}<br><small>Pick ${3 - sel.length} more ingredient${3-sel.length===1?'':'s'}</small></div>`;
    lastCraftKey = ''; return;
  }
  const key = [...sel].sort().join('');
  const hit = RECIPES.find(r => [...r.need].sort().join('') === key);
  if(hit){
    res.innerHTML = `<div><b class="px" style="font-size:12px;color:var(--gold)">${hit.name}</b><br><br>${hit.text}</div>`;
    if(lastCraftKey !== key){
      lastCraftKey = key;
      sfx('level'); unlock('craft'); addXP(15);
    }
  }else{
    res.innerHTML = `<div><b class="px" style="font-size:12px">MYSTERY BREW</b><br><br>Versatility +100. Try a recipe combo!</div>`;
    lastCraftKey = '';
  }
}
$('#craftGrid').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const s = e.target.closest('.cslot'); if(!s) return;
  const c = s.dataset.c;
  if(sel.includes(c)) sel = sel.filter(x => x !== c);
  else { if(sel.length >= 3) sel.shift(); sel.push(c); }
  sfx('click'); renderCraft();
});
$('#craftBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { sel = []; sfx('click'); renderCraft(); });

$('#mailForm').addEventListener('submit', e => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const n = $('#mName').value.trim();
  const em = $('#mEmail').value.trim();
  const msg = $('#mMsg').value.trim();
  const subject = encodeURIComponent('Portfolio message from ' + n);
  const body = encodeURIComponent(`${msg}\n\n— ${n} (${em})`);
  sfx('pop');
  toast('✉️', 'LETTER READY', 'Your email app should open with your message.');
  unlock('mail');
  location.href = `mailto:${CONTACT_EMAIL}?subject=${subject}&body=${body}`;
  e.target.reset();
});

/* ================= HUD / CLOCK / DEBUG ================= */
function texasTime(){
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US',{timeZone:'America/Chicago',hour:'numeric',minute:'2-digit'}).format(new Date());
}
function updateHUD(){
  $('#clock').textContent = texasTime() + ' CT';
  if(document.body.classList.contains('f3')){
    const t = timeInfo();
    $('#debug').textContent =
`SADIE SINK · FAN BUILD
Position: Brenham, TX (30.17°N, 96.40°W)
Local time: ${texasTime()} CT
Daylight: ${(clamp(Math.sin((t-.25)*Math.PI*2)*4+.5,0,1)*100).toFixed(0)}%
Rain: ${rainOn ? 'on' : 'off'} · Night override: ${timeOverride===null?'real time':'forced'}
FPS: ${fps}
Level: ${lvl()} · XP: ${xp}
Mined: ${minedTotal} · Facts: ${factsFound.length}/${FACTS.length}
Advancements: ${ach.length}/${ACH.length}`;
  }
}
setInterval(() => { fps = fpsCount; fpsCount = 0; }, 1000);
setInterval(updateHUD, 500);

function toggleUpside(force){
  const on = force ?? !document.body.classList.contains('upside');
  document.body.classList.toggle('upside', on);
  $('#btnUp').style.borderColor = on ? '#ff4040' : '';
  $('.hearts').classList.remove('shake'); void $('.hearts').offsetWidth; $('.hearts').classList.add('shake');
  sfx(on ? 'low' : 'pop');
  toast('🌀', on ? 'UPSIDE DOWN' : 'BACK TO HAWKINS', on ? 'Something is watching. Stay close to the light.' : 'The gate is closed for now.');
  if(on) unlock('upside');
}
function toggleRain(){
  rainOn = !rainOn;
  $('#btnRain').style.borderColor = rainOn ? '#55ffff' : '';
  sfx('pop'); toast('🌧️', rainOn ? 'RAIN' : 'CLEAR SKY', rainOn ? 'Weather changed. Clouds are moving faster.' : 'The sky is clear again.');
}
function cycleNight(){
  if(timeOverride === null) timeOverride = .5;
  else if(timeOverride === .5) timeOverride = .95;
  else timeOverride = null;
  const msg = timeOverride === null ? 'Real Texas time' : timeOverride === .5 ? 'Forced daytime' : 'Forced nighttime';
  toast('🌗', 'TIME', msg); sfx('click');
}
function toggleSound(){
  soundOn = !soundOn; store.set('sound', soundOn);
  $('#btnSound').textContent = soundOn ? '🔊' : '🔇';
  if(soundOn) sfx('click');
}
$('#btnUp').addEventListener('click', () => toggleUpside());
$('#btnRain').addEventListener('click', toggleRain);
$('#btnSound').addEventListener('click', toggleSound);

document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  if(e.key === 'Escape'){ closeModal(); return; }
  if(/INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT/.test(e.target.tagName)) return;
  if(e.key === 'F3'){ e.preventDefault(); document.body.classList.toggle('f3'); return; }
  const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
  if(k === 'u') toggleUpside();
  else if(k === 'r') toggleRain();
  else if(k === 'm') toggleSound();
  else if(k === 'n') cycleNight();
  else if(/^[1-9]$/.test(e.key)){
    const sec = secs[+e.key - 1];
    if(sec) sec.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'});
  }
});

/* ================= SCROLL: HOTBAR + PROGRESS + ACHIEVEMENT ================= */
const secs = ['home','about','quest','chest','enchant','frames','trophy','craft','mail'].map(id => document.getElementById(id));
let ticking = false;
function onScroll(){
  if(ticking) return; ticking = true;
  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    ticking = false;
    const y = scrollY, max = document.documentElement.scrollHeight - innerHeight;
    $('#progress').style.width = (max > 0 ? y/max*100 : 0) + '%';
    let cur = 0;
    secs.forEach((s,i) => { if(s.getBoundingClientRect().top < innerHeight*.45) cur = i; });
    $$('#hotbar .slot').forEach((s,i) => s.classList.toggle('on', i === cur));
    if(y > innerHeight*.6) unlock('hello');
  });
}
addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, {passive:true});

/* ================= REVEAL + COUNTERS + BARS ================= */
const io = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
  entries.forEach(en => {
    if(!en.isIntersecting) return;
    const el = en.target;
    el.classList.add('in');
    if(el.dataset.to) countUp(el, +el.dataset.to);
    if(el.matches('.bar2 i')) el.style.width = el.dataset.w + '%';
    io.unobserve(el);
  });
}, {threshold:.2});
function countUp(el, target){
  const t0 = performance.now(), dur = 1200;
  (function step(now){
    const p = clamp((now-t0)/dur, 0, 1);
    el.textContent = Math.round(target * (1 - Math.pow(1-p,3)));
    if(p < 1) requestAnimationFrame(step);
  })(t0);
}

/* ================= LOADER & BOOT ================= */
function bootContent(){
  const age = Math.floor((Date.now() - new Date(2002,3,16)) / (365.25*864e5));
  const yrs = new Date().getFullYear() - 2012;
  const roles = WORKS.length;
  const s1 = $('#statAge'), s2 = $('#statYrs'), s3 = $('#statRoles'), s4 = $('#statSeasons');
  s1.dataset.to = age; s2.dataset.to = yrs; s3.dataset.to = roles; s4.dataset.to = 4;
  renderBook(0);
  renderAch();
  renderChest();
  renderFrames();
  renderCraft();
  renderXP();
  $('#btnSound').textContent = soundOn ? '🔊' : '🔇';
  // reveal elements + observe counters/bars
  $$('.reveal, [data-to], .bar2 i').forEach(el => io.observe(el));
}

let lp = 0;
const msgs = ['Generating Brenham, Texas...','Mining Broadway blocks...','Opening the Upside Down gate...','Crafting Max Mayfield...','Loading skateboards...'];
const li = setInterval(() => {
  lp += rnd(3,9);
  if(lp >= 100){
    lp = 100; clearInterval(li);
    setTimeout(hideLoader, 400);
  }
  $('#ldBar').style.width = lp + '%';
  $('#ldTxt').textContent = msgs[Math.min(msgs.length-1, Math.floor(lp/21))];
}, 120);

function hideLoader(){
  $('#loader').classList.add('done');
  resizeWorld();
  drawWorld();
  typer();
  loadWiki();
  setTimeout(() => toast('🎮', 'WORLD LOADED', 'Welcome, SadieSink_2002. Press 1–9 to explore.'), 900);
}
let rzT;
addEventListener('resize', () => { clearTimeout(rzT); rzT = setTimeout(resizeWorld, 150); });

bootContent();
onScroll();
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

## Verify before publishing

- **Tony nomination**: I'm not certain of the exact category or whether she was nominated. The page is marked "verify category."
- **Critics' Choice**: the nomination category for *The Whale* is marked for verification.
- **Blue Bloods and The Americans**: check IMDb for exact episodes and years.
- **O'Dessa**: confirm the premiere, distribution, and description.
- **Spider-Man: Brand New Day**: the release date is widely reported, but her role has not been officially confirmed.
- **Photo rights**: the Wikipedia image is shown with a license note. Use only images you have the right to publish.
- **Contact email**: replace `your-email@example.com` in the script with your real address.

## Tips

- Press **1–9** to jump sections, **F3** for the debug overlay, **N** to force day or night, and **U** for Upside Down.
- Upload a photo in the hero or drop images onto the gallery frames. They stay on your device and are not uploaded anywhere.
- Mine the ground in the hero to earn XP and unlock facts. Your progress saves in the browser.








this is cool but i expect more now make this 10000 times better








Here's a much bigger rebuild. It has a playable pixel world with a walking character, mining with an inventory, a real-time Texas day/night cycle, an interactive US map, a canvas radar chart, a command console, a crafting system, achievements, saved uploads, and a downloadable press kit.

I can't browse the live web from here, so facts come from my training data. Items marked **verify** need checking before you publish.

## What's new

- **Playable hero world**: click the ground to walk your pixel character (Max-style redhead), click nearby ground to mine. Blocks regrow, drop into your inventory, and reveal facts.
- **Real time**: the sky follows Central Time (Brenham, TX). Override with `time day|night|real`.
- **Weather**: rain and clouds, toggled with `R` or the HUD button.
- **Upside Down mode** (`U`): recolors the whole site, the world, and the map.
- **Command console** (`/` or `T`): commands like `goto map`, `fact`, `stats`, `skin reset`.
- **Interactive US map**: visit all five pins to unlock an advancement. Route line from Brenham to New York to Los Angeles.
- **Advancement tree**: connected nodes for Stage, Screen, and Music, with locked and unlocked states.
- **Ender chest**: search, type filter, sort by year or rarity, take items to collect them.
- **Enchanting radar chart** plus skill bars.
- **Photo system**: uploads are compressed and saved in your browser. Frames support drag and drop.
- **Crafting table** with five recipes and a recipe book.
- **Mailbox** with a subject picker, character counter, copy-to-clipboard, and a `mailto` send.
- **Press kit download** as a `.txt` file.
- **Accessibility**: keyboard navigation, focus rings, reduced-motion support, ARIA labels.

```html
<script>
/* ================= 1. CORE ================= */
const $=(s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);
const $$=(s,r=document)=>[...r.querySelectorAll(s)];
const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
const rnd=(a,b)=>a+Math.random()*(b-a);
const sleep=ms=>new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,ms));
const esc=s=>String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const mix=(a,b,t)=>a.map((v,i)=>v+(b[i]-v)*t);
const rgb=c=>`rgb(${c[0]|0},${c[1]|0},${c[2]|0})`;
const hash=(x,y)=>{let n=Math.imul(x,73856093)^Math.imul(y,19349663);n=Math.imul(n^(n>>>13),1274126177);return((n>>>0)%1000)/1000};

const CONFIG={email:'hello@example.com'}; // <- set the address that receives mail
const FR=[{cap:'2012 · Annie (Broadway)'},{cap:'2015 · The Audience'},{cap:'2017 · Hawkins, Season 2'},{cap:'2021 · Fear Street'},{cap:'2022 · The Whale'},{cap:'2025 · O’Dessa'}];
const SEC=['home','lore','map','quest','chest','skills','frames','trophy','craft','mail'];

const store={
  get(k,d){try{const v=localStorage.getItem('sss_'+k);return v===null?d:JSON.parse(v)}catch(e){return d}},
  set(k,v){try{localStorage.setItem('sss_'+k,JSON.stringify(v));return true}catch(e){return false}},
  clearAll(){Object.keys(localStorage).filter(k=>k.startsWith('sss_')).forEach(k=>localStorage.removeItem(k))}
};

const S={
  xp:store.get('xp',0), ach:store.get('ach',[]), inv:store.get('inv',{grass:0,dirt:0,stone:0}),
  mined:store.get('mined',0), facts:store.get('facts',[]), chest:store.get('chest',[]),
  chapters:store.get('chapters',[]), pins:store.get('pins',[]), crafts:store.get('crafts',[]),
  frames:store.get('frames',FR.map(()=>null)), skin:store.get('skin',null), sound:store.get('sound',true),
  rain:false, upside:false, timeOverride:null
};
if(!Array.isArray(S.frames)||S.frames.length!==FR.length) S.frames=FR.map(()=>null);

/* ================= 2. AUDIO / TOASTS / XP / ACHIEVEMENTS ================= */
let actx=null;
function beep(freq,dur=.08,type='square',vol=.05,delay=0){
  if(!S.sound) return;
  try{
    actx=actx||new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();
    if(actx.state==='suspended') actx.resume();
    const t=actx.currentTime+delay, o=actx.createOscillator(), gn=actx.createGain();
    o.type=type; o.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq,t);
    gn.gain.setValueAtTime(vol,t); gn.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+dur);
    o.connect(gn).connect(actx.destination); o.start(t); o.stop(t+dur+.02);
  }catch(e){}
}
const SFX={
  click:()=>beep(440,.04),
  pop:()=>{beep(600,.06);beep(900,.08,'square',.05,.06)},
  mine:()=>{beep(180,.08);beep(120,.1,'sawtooth',.05,.03)},
  level:()=>[523,659,784,1046].forEach((f,i)=>beep(f,.15,'square',.05,i*.1)),
  toast:()=>beep(988,.1,'triangle',.05),
  low:()=>beep(90,.4,'sawtooth',.06)
};

function toast(icon,title,msg,sound=true){
  const box=$('#toasts'), d=document.createElement('div');
  d.className='toast';
  d.innerHTML=`<div class="ti">${icon}</div><div><b>${esc(title)}</b><span>${esc(msg)}</span></div>`;
  box.appendChild(d);
  if(sound) SFX.toast();
  while(box.children.length>4) box.firstElementChild.remove();
  setTimeout(()=>{d.classList.add('out');setTimeout(()=>d.remove(),450)},3800);
}

const level=()=>Math.floor(S.xp/200)+1;
function renderHUD(){
  $('#lvl').textContent=level();
  const into=S.xp%200;
  $('#xpTxt').textContent=`${into} / 200 XP`;
  $('#xpBar').style.width=(into/200*100)+'%';
}
function addXP(n){
  const before=level();
  S.xp+=n; store.set('xp',S.xp); renderHUD();
  if(level()>before){SFX.level(); toast('⭐','LEVEL UP!','You reached level '+level()+'.',false);}
}

const ACHS=[
  {id:'hello',i:'🏠',n:'Hello, Hawkins',d:'Scroll into the world.'},
  {id:'walk',i:'🚶',n:'First Steps',d:'Walk your character across the world.'},
  {id:'dig10',i:'⛏️',n:'Hole Digger',d:'Mine 25 blocks of ground.'},
  {id:'fact',i:'📜',n:'Fact Finder',d:'Discover 5 facts.'},
  {id:'lore',i:'📖',n:'Full Lore',d:'Read all five lore chapters.'},
  {id:'map',i:'🗺️',n:'Cartographer',d:'Visit all five map pins.'},
  {id:'chest',i:'📦',n:'Chest Looter',d:'Collect 4 items from the ender chest.'},
  {id:'craft',i:'⚒️',n:'Master Performance',d:'Discover a crafting recipe.'},
  {id:'frame',i:'🖼️',n:'Picture Perfect',d:'Upload a photo.'},
  {id:'upside',i:'🌀',n:'Upside Down',d:'Enter the Upside Down.'},
  {id:'console',i:'💻',n:'Command Block',d:'Run a console command.'},
  {id:'mail',i:'✉️',n:'Dear Max',d:'Send a letter.'}
];
function unlock(id){
  if(S.ach.includes(id)) return;
  S.ach.push(id); store.set('ach',S.ach);
  const a=ACHS.find(x=>x.id===id);
  toast(a.i,'ADVANCEMENT MADE!',a.n);
  addXP(30); renderAch();
}
function renderAch(){
  $('#achGrid').innerHTML=ACHS.map(a=>{
    const got=S.ach.includes(a.id);
    return `<div class="ach-card ${got?'got':''}"><div class="ai">${got?a.i:'❔'}</div><div><h6>${esc(got?a.n:'???')}</h6><p>${esc(a.d)}</p></div></div>`;
  }).join('');
  $('#achCount').textContent=S.ach.length;
}

/* ================= 3. PARTICLES ================= */
const fxC=$('#fx'), fx=fxC.getContext('2d');
let parts=[];
function fitFx(){fxC.width=innerWidth;fxC.height=innerHeight}
fitFx(); addEventListener('resize',fitFx);
function burst(x,y,cols,n=12){
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++) parts.push({x,y,vx:rnd(-3,3),vy:rnd(-7,-2),life:1,c:cols[i%cols.length],s:rnd(4,8)|0});
}
function fxStep(){
  fx.clearRect(0,0,fxC.width,fxC.height);
  if(!parts.length) return;
  parts=parts.filter(p=>p.life>0);
  for(const p of parts){
    p.vy+=.4; p.x+=p.vx; p.y+=p.vy; p.life-=.025;
    fx.globalAlpha=Math.max(0,p.life); fx.fillStyle=p.c;
    fx.fillRect(p.x|0,p.y|0,p.s,p.s);
  }
  fx.globalAlpha=1;
}
document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
  const el=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('.mine');
  if(el) burst(e.clientX,e.clientY,['#5EBC2E','#79553A','#8a8a8a','#FFEB3B'],10);
});

/* ================= 4. HERO WORLD ================= */
const cv=$('#world'), g=cv.getContext('2d');
const bgc=document.createElement('canvas'), bgx=bgc.getContext('2d');
let W=320,H=180,base=[],terrain=[],trees=[],clouds=[],stars=[],drops=[],flies=[];
const P={x:90,tx:90,dir:1,step:0,swing:0};
let frameN=0, heroVisible=true, curT=0;

const PAL={
  day:{g1:'#5EBC2E',g2:'#4fae22',d1:'#79553A',d2:'#6b4a31',s:['#6f6f6f','#8a8a8a','#7a7a7a'],deep:'#2e2e2e',ore:'#d9b44a',leaf:['#2f7a1c','#3f9a2a'],trunk:'#5b3b1d',sky:[[106,176,255],[190,226,255]],night:[[8,12,36],[28,38,80]],hills:[40,60,90]},
  up:{g1:'#5a1414',g2:'#430f0f',d1:'#2c1010',d2:'#240b0b',s:['#3b2222','#4a2a2a','#432626'],deep:'#1a0a0a',ore:'#ff5a3a',leaf:['#4a0c0c','#5e1414'],trunk:'#2a0b0b',sky:[[40,6,10],[96,18,22]],night:[[40,6,10],[96,18,22]],hills:[60,16,20]}
};
const pal=()=>PAL[S.upside?'up':'day'];
const COL={hair:'#B5362B',hairD:'#7A1F18',skin:'#F2C29B',eye:'#1a1a1a',shirt:'#3d7ddb',shirtD:'#2a5aa3',jeans:'#2f3f6f',boot:'#3b2a1a',belt:'#222'};

function resizeWorld(){
  const r=cv.getBoundingClientRect();
  if(!r.width||!r.height) return;
  H=Math.round(clamp(W*r.height/r.width,150,260));
  cv.width=W; cv.height=H; bgc.width=W; bgc.height=H;
  genWorld();
}
function genWorld(){
  const s=rnd(0,10);
  base=[];
  for(let x=0;x<W;x++) base[x]=Math.round(H*.7+Math.sin(x*.04+s)*H*.05+Math.sin(x*.11+s*2)*H*.018);
  terrain=base.slice();
  trees=Array.from({length:14},()=>({x:Math.floor(rnd(6,W-6)),h:Math.floor(rnd(5,9))}));
  clouds=Array.from({length:8},()=>({x:rnd(0,W),y:rnd(H*.08,H*.34),w:Math.floor(rnd(14,32)),s:rnd(.04,.14)}));
  stars=Array.from({length:90},()=>({x:Math.random()*W,y:Math.random()*H*.6,a:Math.random()*6.28}));
  drops=Array.from({length:110},()=>({x:Math.random()*W,y:Math.random()*H,v:rnd(1.6,3.2)}));
  flies=Array.from({length:22},()=>({x:Math.random()*W,y:H*rnd(.55,.85),p:Math.random()*6.28}));
  P.x=clamp(P.x,4,W-5); P.tx=P.x;
  rebuildBG();
}
function drawColumn(x){
  const p=pal(), t=terrain[x];
  bgx.clearRect(x,0,1,H);
  for(let y=t;y<H;y++){
    const d=y-t, n=hash(x,y);
    let c;
    if(d===0) c=n<.5?p.g1:p.g2;
    else if(d<3) c=n<.5?p.d1:p.d2;
    else if(n>.975) c=p.ore;
    else if(n>.95) c=p.deep;
    else c=n<.2?p.s[0]:n<.4?p.s[1]:p.s[2];
    bgx.fillStyle=c; bgx.fillRect(x,y,1,1);
  }
}
function rebuildBG(){ for(let x=0;x<W;x++) drawColumn(x); }
function blockAt(x){ const d=terrain[x]-base[x]; return d===0?'grass':d<=2?'dirt':'stone'; }
function mine(x){
  if(x<0||x>=W||terrain[x]>=H-6) return null;
  const type=blockAt(x);
  terrain[x]++;
  drawColumn(x);
  S.inv[type]=(S.inv[type]||0)+1; S.mined++;
  store.set('inv',S.inv); store.set('mined',S.mined);
  renderInv(); P.swing=12; SFX.mine();
  return type;
}
function renderInv(){
  $('#invG').textContent=S.inv.grass||0;
  $('#invD').textContent=S.inv.dirt||0;
  $('#invS').textContent=S.inv.stone||0;
  $('#mineCount').textContent=S.mined;
}
setInterval(()=>{
  for(let x=0;x<W;x++) if(terrain[x]>base[x]){ terrain[x]--; drawColumn(x); }
},2500);

cv.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
  const r=cv.getBoundingClientRect();
  const cx=Math.floor((e.clientX-r.left)/r.width*W), cy=Math.floor((e.clientY-r.top)/r.height*H);
  if(cx<0||cx>=W) return;
  const t=terrain[cx];
  if(Math.abs(cx-P.x)<=4.5 && cy>=t && cy<=t+3){
    const type=mine(cx);
    if(type){
      const cols=type==='grass'?['#5EBC2E','#3d8a1c']:type==='dirt'?['#79553A','#4e3320']:['#8a8a8a','#5a5a5a'];
      burst(e.clientX,e.clientY,cols,12);
      addXP(2);
      if(S.mined>=25) unlock('dig10');
      if(Math.random()<.12) revealFact();
    }
  }else{
    P.tx=clamp(cx,3,W-4);
    unlock('walk');
  }
});

function drawOrb(x,y,col,r,a){
  if(a<=0) return;
  g.globalAlpha=a; g.fillStyle=col;
  const cx=Math.round(x), cy=Math.round(y);
  for(let dx=-r;dx<=r;dx++) for(let dy=-r;dy<=r;dy++) if(dx*dx+dy*dy<=r*r) g.fillRect(cx+dx,cy+dy,1,1);
  g.globalAlpha=1;
}
function drawSprite(c,px,gy,dir,step,swing){
  const x=Math.round(px)-5, y=gy-15, sw=Math.sin(step), L=sw<0?1:0, R=sw>0?1:0;
  const f=(col,a,b,w,h)=>{c.fillStyle=col;c.fillRect(a,b,w,h)};
  f('rgba(0,0,0,.25)',x+1,gy,8,1);
  f(COL.hairD,x+2,y,6,2); f(COL.hair,x+2,y,6,1);
  f(COL.hair,x+2,y+2,1,3); f(COL.hair,x+7,y+2,1,3);
  f(COL.hair,x+3,y+2,4,1);
  f(COL.skin,x+3,y+3,4,3);
  f(COL.eye,x+4,y+4,1,1); f(COL.eye,x+6,y+4,1,1);
  f(COL.shirt,x+2,y+6,6,4);
  f(COL.shirtD,x+1,y+6,1,4);
  if(swing>0) f(COL.shirtD,x+8,y+3,1,4); else f(COL.shirtD,x+8,y+6,1,4);
  f(COL.belt,x+2,y+10,6,1);
  f(COL.jeans,x+2,y+11-L,2,3); f(COL.jeans,x+6,y+11-R,2,3);
  f(COL.boot,x+2,y+14-L,2,1); f(COL.boot,x+6,y+14-R,2,1);
}
function updatePlayer(){
  const dx=P.tx-P.x;
  if(Math.abs(dx)>.25){ P.x+=Math.sign(dx)*Math.min(Math.abs(dx),.65); P.dir=Math.sign(dx); P.step+=.32; }
  else { P.x=P.tx; P.step=0; }
  if(P.swing>0) P.swing--;
}
function readTime(){
  if(S.timeOverride!==null) return S.timeOverride;
  const p=new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US',{timeZone:'America/Chicago',hour:'numeric',minute:'numeric',hourCycle:'h23'}).formatToParts(new Date());
  const h=+p.find(x=>x.type==='hour').value%24, m=+p.find(x=>x.type==='minute').value;
  return (h+m/60)/24;
}
function drawWorld(){
  frameN++;
  updatePlayer();
  const p=pal(), up=S.upside;
  const day=clamp(Math.sin((curT-.25)*Math.PI*2)*4+.5,0,1);

  const gr=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,H);
  gr.addColorStop(0,rgb(mix(p.night[0],p.sky[0],day)));
  gr.addColorStop(1,rgb(mix(p.night[1],p.sky[1],day)));
  g.fillStyle=gr; g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);

  if(!up && day<.95){
    for(const s of stars){
      g.globalAlpha=(1-day)*(.45+.45*Math.sin(frameN*.03+s.a));
      g.fillStyle='#fff'; g.fillRect(s.x|0,s.y|0,1,1);
    }
    g.globalAlpha=1;
  }

  const ph=(curT-.25)*Math.PI*2;
  drawOrb(W/2-Math.cos(ph)*W*.42, H*.72-Math.sin(ph)*H*.62, up?'#ff5a3a':'#FFF3A0', 5, up?.8:clamp(day*2,0,1));
  drawOrb(W/2+Math.cos(ph)*W*.42, H*.72+Math.sin(ph)*H*.62, '#E6ECFF', 4, clamp((1-day)*2,0,1));

  g.fillStyle=up?'rgba(90,20,20,.6)':`rgba(255,255,255,${(.3+.45*day).toFixed(2)})`;
  for(const c of clouds){
    c.x+=c.s*(S.rain?.5:1);
    if(c.x>W+c.w) c.x=-c.w-2;
    g.fillRect(c.x|0,c.y|0,c.w,3);
    g.fillRect((c.x+4)|0,(c.y-2)|0,Math.max(1,c.w-8),2);
  }

  g.fillStyle=up?'#2a0b0b':rgb(mix([40,60,90],[120,176,120],day));
  const o=frameN*.004;
  for(let x=0;x<W;x++){
    const hh=H*.56+Math.sin((x+o)*.035)*H*.05+Math.sin((x+o)*.09)*H*.02;
    g.fillRect(x,hh|0,1,H);
  }

  g.drawImage(bgc,0,0);

  for(const tr of trees){
    const gy=terrain[tr.x]; if(gy===undefined) continue;
    g.fillStyle=p.trunk;
    for(let k=1;k<=tr.h;k++) g.fillRect(tr.x,gy-k,1,1);
    const cy=gy-tr.h-2;
    for(let dy=-3;dy<=2;dy++) for(let dx=-3;dx<=3;dx++){
      if(Math.abs(dx)+Math.abs(dy)>4) continue;
      g.fillStyle=hash(tr.x+dx,cy+dy)<.5?p.leaf[0]:p.leaf[1];
      g.fillRect(tr.x+dx,cy+dy,1,1);
    }
  }

  if(!up && day<.8){
    g.fillStyle='#ffef7a';
    for(const f of flies){
      g.globalAlpha=(1-day)*(.5+.5*Math.sin(frameN*.05+f.p));
      g.fillRect((f.x+Math.sin(frameN*.01+f.p)*3)|0,(f.y+Math.cos(frameN*.013+f.p)*2)|0,1,1);
    }
    g.globalAlpha=1;
  }

  const gx=clamp(Math.round(P.x),0,W-1);
  drawSprite(g,P.x,terrain[gx],P.dir,P.step,P.swing);

  if(!up){ g.fillStyle=`rgba(4,8,30,${(1-day)*.35})`; g.fillRect(0,0,W,H); }

  if(S.rain){
    g.fillStyle='rgba(190,215,255,.5)';
    for(const d of drops){
      g.fillRect(d.x|0,d.y|0,1,3);
      d.y+=d.v*2; d.x-=.5;
      if(d.y>H){d.y=-3;d.x=Math.random()*W;}
      if(d.x<0) d.x=W-1;
    }
  }
}

/* ================= 5. TIME / HUD / TOGGLES ================= */
function updateTime(){ curT=readTime(); }
function clockText(){
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US',{timeZone:'America/Chicago',hour:'numeric',minute:'2-digit'}).format(new Date())+' CT'+(S.timeOverride!==null?' · forced':'');
}
setInterval(()=>{
  updateTime();
  $('#clock').textContent=clockText();
  if(document.body.classList.contains('f3')){
    const day=clamp(Math.sin((curT-.25)*Math.PI*2)*4+.5,0,1);
    $('#debug').textContent=
`SADIE SINK · FAN BUILD
Position: Brenham, TX (30.17N, 96.40W)
Local time: ${clockText()}
Daylight: ${(day*100).toFixed(0)}%
Rain: ${S.rain?'on':'off'} · Upside Down: ${S.upside?'on':'off'}
Player x: ${P.x.toFixed(1)} · Mined: ${S.mined}
Level: ${level()} · XP: ${S.xp}
Facts: ${S.facts.length}/${FACTS.length} · Advancements: ${S.ach.length}/${ACHS.length}`;
  }
},1000);

function toggleUpside(force){
  S.upside=typeof force==='boolean'?force:!S.upside;
  document.body.classList.toggle('upside',S.upside);
  $('#btnUp').classList.toggle('on',S.upside);
  rebuildBG();
  (S.upside?SFX.low:SFX.pop)();
  toast('🌀',S.upside?'UPSIDE DOWN':'BACK TO HAWKINS',S.upside?'Something is watching. Stay close to the light.':'The gate is closed for now.',false);
  if(S.upside) unlock('upside');
}
function toggleRain(){
  S.rain=!S.rain;
  $('#btnRain').classList.toggle('on',S.rain);
  SFX.pop();
  toast('🌧️',S.rain?'RAIN':'CLEAR SKY',S.rain?'Clouds are moving faster.':'The sky is clear again.',false);
}
function cycleTime(){
  if(S.timeOverride===null) S.timeOverride=.5;
  else if(S.timeOverride===.5) S.timeOverride=.95;
  else S.timeOverride=null;
  updateTime();
  toast('🌗','TIME',S.timeOverride===null?'Real Texas time':S.timeOverride===.5?'Forced daytime':'Forced nighttime',false);
  SFX.click();
}
function toggleSound(){
  S.sound=!S.sound; store.set('sound',S.sound);
  $('#btnSound').textContent=S.sound?'🔊':'🔇';
  if(S.sound) SFX.click();
}
$('#btnUp').addEventListener('click',()=>toggleUpside());
$('#btnRain').addEventListener('click',toggleRain);
$('#btnSound').addEventListener('click',toggleSound);
$('#btnCon').addEventListener('click',()=>openCon());

/* ================= 6. FACTS ================= */
const FACTS=[
  'Sadie Elizabeth Sink was born April 16, 2002, in Brenham, Texas.',
  'At 10, she was cast as Annie on Broadway at the Palace Theatre (2012–2014).',
  'In 2015 she played the young Queen Elizabeth II in The Audience, opposite Dame Helen Mirren.',
  'Max Mayfield joined Stranger Things in Season 2 in 2017.',
  'Max’s Season 4 storyline used Kate Bush’s “Running Up That Hill”.',
  'She played Ziggy Berman in the Netflix Fear Street trilogy (2021).',
  'In A24’s The Whale (2022), she played Ellie opposite Brendan Fraser.',
  'Spider-Man: Brand New Day is scheduled for July 31, 2026. Her role is not officially confirmed.'
];
function revealFact(){
  const i=Math.floor(Math.random()*FACTS.length);
  if(!S.facts.includes(i)){S.facts.push(i);store.set('facts',S.facts);}
  toast('📜',`FACT ${S.facts.length}/${FACTS.length}`,FACTS[i]);
  addXP(5);
  if(S.facts.length>=5) unlock('fact');
}

/* ================= 7. HERO TYPER / PORTRAIT ================= */
const LINES=['Actress.','Broadway veteran.','Max Mayfield, Hawkins.','Scream queen.','Singer & guitarist.','Spider-Man: Brand New Day (2026).'];
async function typer(){
  const el=$('#tt'); let i=0;
  while(true){
    const L=LINES[i%LINES.length];
    for(let c=0;c<=L.length;c++){el.textContent=L.slice(0,c);await sleep(60)}
    await sleep(1400);
    for(let c=L.length;c>=0;c--){el.textContent=L.slice(0,c);await sleep(28)}
    await sleep(250); i++;
  }
}

function setSkin(src,credit){
  const img=$('#skinImg'), fb=$('#skinFb');
  img.onload=()=>{img.hidden=false;fb.hidden=true};
  img.onerror=()=>{img.hidden=true;fb.hidden=false};
  img.src=src;
  $('#credit').textContent=credit;
}
let wikiSkinUrl=null;
async function loadWiki(){
  try{
    const r=await fetch('https://en.wikipedia.org/api/rest_v1/page/summary/Sadie_Sink');
    if(!r.ok) throw new Error('bad');
    const j=await r.json();
    if(j.extract) $('#wikiBlurb').textContent=j.extract;
    const src=(j.originalimage||j.thumbnail||{}).source;
    if(src){
      wikiSkinUrl=src;
      if(!S.skin) setSkin(src,'Photo: Wikipedia · check the license · upload your own to replace');
    }else if(!S.skin){ $('#credit').textContent='No photo found · upload your own'; }
  }catch(e){
    $('#wikiBlurb').textContent='Live Wikipedia summary unavailable (offline or blocked). The built-in lore below still works.';
    if(!S.skin) $('#credit').textContent='Offline · upload a photo to personalize';
  }
}
function compress(file,max=700){
  return new Promise((res,rej)=>{
    const img=new Image(), url=URL.createObjectURL(file);
    img.onload=()=>{
      const s=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.width,img.height));
      const c=document.createElement('canvas');
      c.width=Math.round(img.width*s); c.height=Math.round(img.height*s);
      c.getContext('2d').drawImage(img,0,0,c.width,c.height);
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url); res(c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',.82));
    };
    img.onerror=()=>{URL.revokeObjectURL(url);rej(new Error('bad image'))};
    img.src=url;
  });
}
$('#skinBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>$('#skinIn').click());
$('#skinIn').addEventListener('change',async e=>{
  const f=e.target.files[0]; e.target.value='';
  if(!f||!f.type.startsWith('image/')) return toast('⚠️','BAD FILE','Choose an image file.');
  try{
    const d=await compress(f,700);
    S.skin=d;
    if(!store.set('skin',d)) toast('⚠️','STORAGE FULL','Photo will reset when you leave the page.');
    setSkin(d,'Your photo · saved in this browser');
    toast('📷','SKIN UPDATED','Your photo is now the player skin.');
    addXP(15); unlock('frame');
  }catch(err){ toast('⚠️','BAD IMAGE','That file could not be read.'); }
});

function updateLoreBar(){
  $('#loreBar').style.width=(S.chapters.length/CHAPTERS.length*100)+'%';
  $('#loreCount').textContent=S.chapters.length;
}
function renderBook(i){
  $('#bookTabs').innerHTML=CHAPTERS.map((c,k)=>`<button class="book-tab${k===i?' on':''}" data-k="${k}" role="tab" aria-selected="${k===i}">${c.t}</button>`).join('');
  const pg=$('#bookPage'), c=CHAPTERS[i];
  pg.innerHTML=`<h3 class="px">${c.h}</h3>${c.html}`;
  pg.classList.remove('flip'); void pg.offsetWidth; pg.classList.add('flip');
  if(!S.chapters.includes(i)){
    S.chapters.push(i); store.set('chapters',S.chapters);
    updateLoreBar();
    if(S.chapters.length===CHAPTERS.length) unlock('lore');
  }
}
$('#bookTabs').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const b=e.target.closest('.book-tab'); if(!b) return;
  SFX.click(); renderBook(+b.dataset.k);
});

/* ================= 9. ADVANCEMENT TREE ================= */
const TREE=[
  {id:'root',x:50,y:7,i:'🌱',t:'Born in Brenham',y0:'2002',d:'Born April 16, 2002, in Brenham, Texas.',got:1},
  {id:'annie',x:18,y:30,i:'🎈',t:'Annie on Broadway',y0:'2012',d:'Cast as Annie at age 10 at the Palace Theatre. The role ran from 2012 to 2014.',got:1,from:'root'},
  {id:'aud',x:18,y:52,i:'👑',t:'The Audience',y0:'2015',d:'Played the young Queen Elizabeth II opposite Dame Helen Mirren.',got:1,from:'annie'},
  {id:'proctor',x:18,y:74,i:'🎭',t:'John Proctor Is the Villain',y0:'2025',d:'Returned to Broadway in Kimberly Belflower’s play as Shelby.',got:1,from:'aud'},
  {id:'glass',x:50,y:30,i:'🏜️',t:'The Glass Castle',y0:'2017',d:'Played young Lori Walls in the adaptation of Jeannette Walls’ memoir.',got:1,from:'root'},
  {id:'st',x:50,y:52,i:'🚲',t:'Stranger Things',y0:'2017–25',d:'Max Mayfield from Season 2 through Season 5.',got:1,from:'glass'},
  {id:'fear',x:50,y:74,i:'🔪',t:'Fear Street',y0:'2021',d:'Ziggy Berman in Netflix’s horror trilogy.',got:1,from:'st'},
  {id:'atw',x:82,y:30,i:'🧣',t:'All Too Well',y0:'2021',d:'Short film co-starring Dylan O’Brien.',got:1,from:'root'},
  {id:'whale',x:82,y:52,i:'🐋',t:'The Whale',y0:'2022',d:'Ellie opposite Brendan Fraser in Darren Aronofsky’s A24 drama.',got:1,from:'atw'},
  {id:'odessa',x:82,y:74,i:'🎸',t:"O'Dessa",y0:'2025',d:'Musical film in which she sings and plays guitar.',got:1,from:'whale'},
  {id:'spider',x:50,y:93,i:'🕷️',t:'Spider-Man: Brand New Day',y0:'2026',d:'Marvel Studios film, scheduled for July 31, 2026. Her role has not been officially revealed.',got:0,from:'fear'}
];
function renderTree(){
  const box=$('#tree'), byId=Object.fromEntries(TREE.map(n=>[n.id,n]));
  const lines=TREE.filter(n=>n.from).map(n=>{
    const p=byId[n.from];
    return `<line x1="${p.x}" y1="${p.y}" x2="${n.x}" y2="${n.y}" class="${n.got?'':'dash'}"/>`;
  }).join('');
  box.innerHTML=`<svg class="tree-svg" viewBox="0 0 100 100" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden="true">${lines}</svg>`+
    TREE.map((n,k)=>`
      <div class="node ${n.got?'got':'lock'}" style="left:${n.x}%;top:${n.y}%" data-k="${k}" tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="${esc(n.t)}">
        <div class="nd-top"><span class="ic">${n.i}</span><span class="yr">${n.y0}</span></div>
        <h4>${esc(n.t)}</h4>
      </div>`).join('');
}
$('#tree').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const n=e.target.closest('.node'); if(!n) return;
  const t=TREE[+n.dataset.k];
  SFX.pop();
  openModal(`${t.i} ${t.t.toUpperCase()}`,`
    <dl><dt>YEAR</dt><dd>${esc(t.y0)}</dd><dt>STATUS</dt><dd>${t.got?'✔ UNLOCKED':'🔒 LOCKED'}</dd></dl>
    <p style="font-size:24px;line-height:1.4">${esc(t.d)}</p>`);
});
$('#tree').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{ if(e.key==='Enter'&&e.target.classList.contains('node')) e.target.click(); });

/* ================= 10. US MAP ================= */
const US=[[-124.7,48.4],[-123.2,49.0],[-95.2,49.0],[-95.2,49.4],[-89.6,48.0],[-84.5,46.5],[-82.4,45.3],[-82.5,43.0],[-79.0,43.3],[-76.5,44.2],[-74.7,45.0],[-71.5,45.0],[-69.2,47.4],[-67.8,47.0],[-67.0,44.8],[-70.2,43.7],[-70.6,41.7],[-74.0,40.6],[-75.5,38.8],[-76.0,37.0],[-75.5,35.2],[-79.0,33.4],[-81.1,31.8],[-81.4,30.0],[-80.0,26.5],[-80.5,25.2],[-81.8,26.2],[-82.8,28.0],[-84.0,30.1],[-86.5,30.4],[-89.5,30.2],[-89.2,29.0],[-91.5,29.5],[-94.7,29.4],[-97.2,27.6],[-97.4,25.9],[-99.1,26.4],[-101.4,29.8],[-103.2,29.0],[-104.5,29.6],[-106.5,31.8],[-108.2,31.8],[-111.0,31.3],[-114.8,32.5],[-117.1,32.5],[-118.5,34.0],[-120.6,34.5],[-121.9,36.6],[-122.5,37.8],[-124.0,40.4],[-124.2,43.0],[-124.0,46.2]];
const PINS=[
  {id:'brenham',name:'Brenham, TX',lat:30.17,lon:-96.40,icon:'🏡',text:'Born April 16, 2002. The small Texas town where Sadie grew up.'},
  {id:'nyc',name:'New York City',lat:40.71,lon:-74.00,icon:'🎭',text:'Broadway: Annie at the Palace Theatre (2012–2014), The Audience (2015), and John Proctor Is the Villain (2025).'},
  {id:'atlanta',name:'Atlanta, GA',lat:33.75,lon:-84.39,icon:'🎬',text:'Stranger Things was largely filmed in the Atlanta area of Georgia.'},
  {id:'hawkins',name:'Hawkins (fiction)',lat:39.8,lon:-86.2,icon:'🚲',text:'Hawkins, Indiana is fictional. This pin marks where the show’s story is set, not a filming location.'},
  {id:'la',name:'Los Angeles',lat:34.05,lon:-118.24,icon:'🌴',text:'A hub for film and TV. Many productions are based in or near Los Angeles.'}
];
const mapC=$('#mapCv'), mg=mapC.getContext('2d');
const MW=720, MH=397;
const mproj=(lon,lat)=>[(lon+125)/59*MW,(50-lat)/26*MH];
let mapVis=false;
function mapDraw(){
  const t=performance.now()/1000, up=S.upside;
  mg.fillStyle=up?'#1a0404':'#14365c'; mg.fillRect(0,0,MW,MH);
  mg.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.07)'; mg.lineWidth=1;
  for(let x=0;x<MW;x+=30){mg.beginPath();mg.moveTo(x,0);mg.lineTo(x,MH);mg.stroke();}
  for(let y=0;y<MH;y+=30){mg.beginPath();mg.moveTo(0,y);mg.lineTo(MW,y);mg.stroke();}
  const pts=US.map(([a,b])=>mproj(a,b));
  mg.beginPath(); pts.forEach(([x,y],i)=>i?mg.lineTo(x,y):mg.moveTo(x,y)); mg.closePath();
  mg.fillStyle=up?'#4a1212':'#4f9a2b'; mg.fill();
  mg.strokeStyle='#000'; mg.lineWidth=4; mg.stroke();

  const route=['brenham','nyc','la'].map(id=>PINS.find(p=>p.id===id));
  mg.setLineDash([12,10]); mg.lineDashOffset=-t*30;
  mg.strokeStyle='#FFD84A'; mg.lineWidth=4; mg.beginPath();
  route.forEach((p,i)=>{const [x,y]=mproj(p.lon,p.lat); i?mg.lineTo(x,y):mg.moveTo(x,y);});
  mg.stroke(); mg.setLineDash([]);

  mg.textAlign='center'; mg.textBaseline='middle';
  for(const p of PINS){
    const [x,y]=mproj(p.lon,p.lat), bob=Math.sin(t*3+x)*2, seen=S.pins.includes(p.id);
    mg.fillStyle='#000'; mg.fillRect(x-14,y-14+bob,28,28);
    mg.fillStyle=seen?'#FFD84A':'#cfcfcf'; mg.fillRect(x-11,y-11+bob,22,22);
    mg.font='16px serif'; mg.fillStyle='#000'; mg.fillText(p.icon,x,y+bob+1);
    mg.font='20px VT323, monospace';
    mg.lineWidth=4; mg.strokeStyle='#000'; mg.strokeText(p.name,x,y+26+bob);
    mg.fillStyle='#fff'; mg.fillText(p.name,x,y+26+bob);
  }
}
mapC.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
  const r=mapC.getBoundingClientRect();
  const x=(e.clientX-r.left)/r.width*MW, y=(e.clientY-r.top)/r.height*MH;
  let best=null, bd=1e9;
  for(const p of PINS){const [px,py]=mproj(p.lon,p.lat); const d=Math.hypot(px-x,py-y); if(d<bd){bd=d;best=p;}}
  if(best&&bd<42) selectPin(best);
});
function selectPin(p){
  SFX.pop();
  if(!S.pins.includes(p.id)){
    S.pins.push(p.id); store.set('pins',S.pins);
    if(S.pins.length===PINS.length) unlock('map');
    addXP(10);
  }
  $('#mapCount').textContent=S.pins.length;
  $('#mapInfo').innerHTML=`<h3 class="px">${p.icon} ${esc(p.name.toUpperCase())}</h3><p>${esc(p.text)}</p>`;
}

/* ================= 11. ENDER CHEST ================= */

  {id:'fear',icon:'🔪',title:'Fear Street Trilogy',y:2021,year:'2021',type:'Film',role:'Ziggy Berman',rarity:'epic',desc:'Netflix horror trilogy shot back to back. Ziggy is a fan-favorite role in her scream-queen run.'},
  {id:'atw',icon:'🧣',title:'All Too Well: The Short Film',y:2021,year:'2021',type:'Short',role:'Younger version of the lead',rarity:'rare',desc:'Taylor Swift’s short film, co-starring Dylan O’Brien.'},
  {id:'whale',icon:'🐋',title:'The Whale',y:2022,year:'2022',type:'Film',role:'Ellie',rarity:'epic',desc:'A24 drama directed by Darren Aronofsky, opposite Brendan Fraser. Her performance drew awards attention.'},
  {id:'odessa',icon:'🎸',title:"O'Dessa",y:2025,year:'2025',type:'Music',role:"O'Dessa",rarity:'epic',desc:'Musical film in which she sings and performs original songs, including on guitar.'},
  {id:'proctor',icon:'🎭',title:'John Proctor Is the Villain',y:2025,year:'2025',type:'Stage',role:'Shelby',rarity:'legendary',desc:'Broadway return in Kimberly Belflower’s play. Recognized with a 2025 Tony nomination (verify category).'},
  {id:'spider',icon:'🕷️',title:'Spider-Man: Brand New Day',y:2026,year:'2026',type:'Film',role:'Under wraps',rarity:'mythic',desc:'Marvel Studios film with Tom Holland, scheduled for July 31, 2026. Her character has not been officially revealed.'}
];
const RC={common:'#cfcfcf',uncommon:'#55ff55',rare:'#55ffff',epic:'#d47bff',legendary:'#ffaa00',mythic:'#ff5555'};
const RANK={common:0,uncommon:1,rare:2,epic:3,legendary:4,mythic:5};
let cFilter='all', cQ='', cSort='new';
function renderChest(){
  const list=WORKS.filter(w=>(cFilter==='all'||w.type===cFilter)&&`${w.title} ${w.role} ${w.year}`.toLowerCase().includes(cQ));
  list.sort((a,b)=>cSort==='new'?b.y-a.y:cSort==='old'?a.y-b.y:RANK[b.rarity]-RANK[a.rarity]);
  $('#chest-grid').innerHTML=list.length?list.map(w=>{
    const taken=S.chest.includes(w.id);
    return `<button class="item${taken?' taken':''}" data-id="${w.id}" style="--rc:${RC[w.rarity]}">
      <span class="rar">${w.rarity}</span>
      <span class="big">${w.icon}</span>
      <h5>${esc(w.title)}</h5>
      <span class="meta">${esc(w.year)} · ${esc(w.type)}</span>
      ${taken?'<span class="took">✔ TAKEN</span>':''}
    </button>`;
  }).join(''):'<p class="px empty">EMPTY CHEST · TRY ANOTHER FILTER</p>';
  $('#chestCount').textContent=`${S.chest.length}/${WORKS.length} collected`;
}
$('.toolbar').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const b=e.target.closest('.filter'); if(!b) return;
  $$('.filter').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('on'));
  b.classList.add('on'); cFilter=b.dataset.f; SFX.click(); renderChest();
});
$('#cSearch').addEventListener('input',e=>{cQ=e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase();renderChest();});
$('#cSort').addEventListener('change',e=>{cSort=e.target.value;renderChest();});
function openWork(w){
  const taken=S.chest.includes(w.id);
  SFX.pop();
  openModal(`${w.icon} ${w.title.toUpperCase()}`,`
    <div class="big">${w.icon}</div>
    <dl>
      <dt>ROLE</dt><dd>${esc(w.role)}</dd>
      <dt>YEAR</dt><dd>${esc(w.year)}</dd>
      <dt>TYPE</dt><dd>${esc(w.type)}</dd>
      <dt>RARITY</dt><dd style="color:${RC[w.rarity]};text-shadow:1px 1px 0 #000">${w.rarity.toUpperCase()}</dd>
    </dl>
    <p style="font-size:24px;line-height:1.4">${esc(w.desc)}</p>
    <div class="actions">
      <button class="btn g sm" id="takeBtn" type="button" ${taken?'disabled':''}>${taken?'✔ ALREADY TAKEN':'🎁 TAKE ITEM'}</button>
      <a class="btn sm" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special:Search?search=${encodeURIComponent(w.title)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">📖 Wikipedia</a>
    </div>`);
  $('#takeBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{
    if(S.chest.includes(w.id)) return;
    S.chest.push(w.id); store.set('chest',S.chest);
    addXP(10); SFX.level();
    toast('📦','ITEM TAKEN',w.title);
    if(S.chest.length>=4) unlock('chest');
    renderChest(); closeModal();
  });
}
$('#chest-grid').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const b=e.target.closest('.item'); if(!b) return;
  openWork(WORKS.find(w=>w.id===b.dataset.id));
});

/* ================= 12. MODAL ================= */
function openModal(title,html){
  $('#mTitle').textContent=title;
  $('#mBody').innerHTML=html;
  $('#modal').classList.add('open');
  document.body.classList.add('locked');
  $('#mClose').focus();
}
function closeModal(){
  if(!$('#modal').classList.contains('open')) return;
  $('#modal').classList.remove('open');
  document.body.classList.remove('locked');
}
$('#mClose').addEventListener('click',()=>{SFX.click();closeModal();});
$('#modal').addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.id==='modal') closeModal();});

/* ================= 13. SKILLS & RADAR ================= */
const SKILLS=[
  ['Dramatic range',97,'Acting'],
  ['Musical theatre',95,'Musical'],
  ['Scream queen',96,'Horror'],
  ['Guitar & vocals',82,'Music'],
  ['Skateboarding (Max)',78,'Skate'],
  ['Versatility',94,'Range']
];
$('#skillBars').innerHTML=SKILLS.map(([n,v])=>`
  <div class="skill">
    <div class="row"><span>${n}</span><span>LV ${v}</span></div>
    <div class="bar gold"><i data-w="${v}"></i></div>
  </div>`).join('');
const rad=$('#radar'), rg=rad.getContext('2d');
function drawRadar(k){
  const Wd=rad.width, Hd=rad.height, cx=Wd/2, cy=Hd/2, R=Wd*.32, N=SKILLS.length;
  const pt=(i,r)=>{const a=-Math.PI/2+i*2*Math.PI/N;return [cx+Math.cos(a)*r,cy+Math.sin(a)*r];};
  rg.clearRect(0,0,Wd,Hd);
  rg.strokeStyle='#3a3a3a'; rg.lineWidth=2;
  for(let ring=1;ring<=4;ring++){
    rg.beginPath();
    for(let i=0;i<N;i++){const [x,y]=pt(i,R*ring/4); i?rg.lineTo(x,y):rg.moveTo(x,y);}
    rg.closePath(); rg.stroke();
  }
  rg.textAlign='center'; rg.textBaseline='middle'; rg.font='24px VT323, monospace'; rg.fillStyle='#fff';
  for(let i=0;i<N;i++){
    const [x,y]=pt(i,R); rg.beginPath(); rg.moveTo(cx,cy); rg.lineTo(x,y); rg.stroke();
    const [lx,ly]=pt(i,R+30); rg.fillText(SKILLS[i][2],lx,ly);
  }
  rg.beginPath();
  SKILLS.forEach((s,i)=>{const [x,y]=pt(i,R*s[1]/100*k); i?rg.lineTo(x,y):rg.moveTo(x,y);});
  rg.closePath();
  rg.fillStyle='rgba(124,252,0,.3)'; rg.fill();
  rg.strokeStyle='#7CFC00'; rg.lineWidth=4; rg.stroke();
}
let radarDone=false;
function animRadar(){
  const t0=performance.now();
  (function step(now){
    const p=clamp((now-t0)/1200,0,1);
    drawRadar(1-Math.pow(1-p,3));
    if(p<1) requestAnimationFrame(step);
  })(t0);
}

/* ================= 14. FRAMES ================= */
let frameTarget=null;
function renderFrames(){
  $('#frameGrid').innerHTML=FR.map((f,k)=>{
    const src=S.frames[k];
    return `<button class="frame" data-k="${k}" type="button" aria-label="${esc(f.cap)}">
      <div class="pic" style="${src?`background-image:url('${src}')`:''}">${src?'':'📷 Click or drop<br>a photo here'}</div>
      <div class="cap">${esc(f.cap)}</div>
    </button>`;
  }).join('');
}
function setFrame(k,file){
  compress(file,640).then(d=>{
    S.frames[k]=d;
    if(!store.set('frames',S.frames)) toast('⚠️','STORAGE FULL','Frame will reset when you leave the page.');
    renderFrames();
    toast('🖼️','ITEM FRAME FILLED',FR[k].cap);
    addXP(10); unlock('frame');
  }).catch(()=>toast('⚠️','BAD IMAGE','That file could not be read.'));
}
$('#frameGrid').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const fr=e.target.closest('.frame'); if(!fr) return;
  const k=+fr.dataset.k;
  if(S.frames[k]){
    openModal(FR[k].cap,`
      <img src="${S.frames[k]}" alt="${esc(FR[k].cap)}">
      <div class="actions">
        <button class="btn sm" id="replaceBtn" type="button">🔁 Replace</button>
        <button class="btn r sm" id="removeBtn" type="button">🗑 Remove</button>
      </div>`);
    $('#replaceBtn').onclick=()=>{frameTarget=k;closeModal();$('#frameIn').click();};
    $('#removeBtn').onclick=()=>{S.frames[k]=null;store.set('frames',S.frames);renderFrames();closeModal();toast('🗑️','REMOVED',FR[k].cap,false);};
  }else{
    frameTarget=k; $('#frameIn').click();
  }
});
$('#frameIn').addEventListener('change',e=>{
  const f=e.target.files[0]; e.target.value='';
  if(f&&frameTarget!==null&&f.type.startsWith('image/')) setFrame(frameTarget,f);
  frameTarget=null;
});
const fgEl=$('#frameGrid');
fgEl.addEventListener('dragover',e=>{
  e.preventDefault();
  $$('.frame').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('drag'));
  const fr=e.target.closest('.frame'); if(fr) fr.classList.add('drag');
});
fgEl.addEventListener('dragleave',()=>$$('.frame').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('drag')));
fgEl.addEventListener('drop',e=>{
  e.preventDefault();
  $$('.frame').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('drag'));
  const fr=e.target.closest('.frame'), f=e.dataTransfer.files[0];
  if(fr&&f&&f.type.startsWith('image/')) setFrame(+fr.dataset.k,f);
});

/* ================= 15. CRAFTING ================= */
const INGR=['🎭','🎸','🛹','😱','👑','🎬','🌀','🎤','🕷️','📖'];
const RECIPES=[
  {need:['🎭','🎸','🛹'],name:"O'DESSA ROCKSTAR",text:'Stage presence + guitar + skate energy.'},
  {need:['😱','🎬','🌀'],name:'SCREAM QUEEN ARMOR',text:'Horror + screen + Upside Down = a Hawkins survivor.'},
  {need:['👑','🎭','🎤'],name:'BROADWAY CROWN',text:'Royalty + stage + microphone.'},
  {need:['🕷️','🛹','🎬'],name:'NEIGHBORHOOD SET',text:'Spider + skate + screen. Brand New Day loading…'},
  {need:['📖','🎭','🎬'],name:'SCRIPT ENCHANTMENT',text:'Book + stage + screen = a character built from the page.'}
];
let sel=[];
const recKey=arr=>[...arr].sort().join('');
function renderCraft(){
  $('#cgrid').innerHTML=INGR.map(c=>`<button class="cslot${sel.includes(c)?' sel':''}" data-c="${c}" type="button" aria-pressed="${sel.includes(c)}">${c}</button>`).join('');
  evalCraft();
}
function evalCraft(){
  const out=$('#cout');
  if(sel.length<3){
    out.innerHTML=`<div>${sel.length?sel.join(' + '):'Empty table'}<br><small style="font-size:20px;color:#bbb">Pick ${3-sel.length} more ingredient${3-sel.length===1?'':'s'}</small></div>`;
    return;
  }
  const hit=RECIPES.find(r=>recKey(r.need)===recKey(sel));
  if(hit){
    out.innerHTML=`<div><b class="px" style="font-size:12px;color:var(--gold)">${hit.name}</b><br><br>${hit.text}</div>`;
    if(!S.crafts.includes(hit.name)){
      S.crafts.push(hit.name); store.set('crafts',S.crafts);
      SFX.level(); unlock('craft'); addXP(15); renderRecipes();
    }
  }else{
    out.innerHTML=`<div><b class="px" style="font-size:12px">MYSTERY BREW</b><br><br>Versatility +100. Try a different combo.</div>`;
  }
}
function renderRecipes(){
  $('#recipes').innerHTML=RECIPES.map(r=>{
    const got=S.crafts.includes(r.name);
    return `<div class="recipe ${got?'got':''}"><h6>${got?r.name:'??? RECIPE'}</h6>${got?`${r.need.join(' ')}<br>${esc(r.text)}`:'Discover this combo at the table.'}</div>`;
  }).join('');
}
$('#cgrid').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const b=e.target.closest('.cslot'); if(!b) return;
  const c=b.dataset.c;
  if(sel.includes(c)) sel=sel.filter(x=>x!==c);
  else{ if(sel.length>=3) sel.shift(); sel.push(c); }
  SFX.click(); renderCraft();
});
$('#cClear').addEventListener('click',()=>{sel=[];SFX.click();renderCraft();});

/* ================= 16. MAIL / PRESS KIT ================= */
const msgEl=$('#mMsg');
msgEl.addEventListener('input',()=>{$('#mCount').textContent=msgEl.value.length;});
$('#mCopy').addEventListener('click',async()=>{
  try{ await navigator.clipboard.writeText(msgEl.value); toast('📋','COPIED','Your message is on the clipboard.'); }
  catch(e){ toast('⚠️','COPY FAILED','Select the text and copy it manually.'); }
});

  SFX.pop();

$('#kit').addEventListener('click',()=>{

Born: April 16, 2002, Brenham, Texas
```
