/* ================= 17. CONSOLE ================= */
const HELP=[
  'Commands:',
  '  help                  show this list',
  '  time day|night|real   set world time',
  '  rain                  toggle weather',
  '  upside                toggle Upside Down',
  '  goto <name|1-9|0>     jump to a section',
  '  mine                  mine the block under you',
  '  fact                  reveal a fact',
  '  stats                 show progress',
  '  ach                   list unlocked advancements',
  '  skin                  upload a new photo (skin reset to restore)',
  '  sound                 toggle sound',
  '  creeper               ssssss…',
  '  reset yes             wipe all saved progress'
];
const ALIAS={home:0,spawn:0,lore:1,book:1,map:2,quest:3,tree:3,chest:4,items:4,skills:5,enchant:5,frames:6,photos:6,trophy:7,awards:7,craft:8,crafting:8,mail:9,contact:9};
function resolveSec(v){
  if(!v) return null;
  if(/^\d$/.test(v)) return SEC[(+v+9)%10];
  if(ALIAS[v]!==undefined) return SEC[ALIAS[v]];
  return null;
}
function addLog(text,cls=''){
  const log=$('#conLog');
  const d=document.createElement('div');
  if(cls) d.className=cls;
  d.textContent=text;
  log.appendChild(d); log.scrollTop=log.scrollHeight;
}
function conRun(line){
  const parts=line.trim().toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if(!parts.length) return;
  const [c,...a]=parts;
  addLog('> '+line.trim(),'cmd');
  unlock('console');
  const out=[];
  switch(c){
    case 'help': out.push(...HELP); break;
    case 'time': {
      const v=a[0];
      if(v==='day') S.timeOverride=.5;
      else if(v==='night') S.timeOverride=.95;
      else if(v==='real') S.timeOverride=null;
      else { out.push('usage: time day|night|real'); break; }
      updateTime(); out.push('time set to '+v); break;
    }
    case 'rain': toggleRain(); out.push('rain '+(S.rain?'on':'off')); break;
    case 'upside': toggleUpside(); out.push('upside '+(S.upside?'on':'off')); break;
    case 'goto': {
      const id=resolveSec(a[0]);
      if(!id){ out.push('unknown section. try: lore, map, chest, craft, mail or 1-9, 0'); break; }
      document.getElementById(SEC[SEC.indexOf(id)]==='home'?'home':SEC[SEC.indexOf(id)]).scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'});
      out.push('going to '+a[0]); break;
    }
    case 'mine': {
      const t=mine(Math.round(P.x));
      out.push(t?`mined a ${t} block`:'nothing to mine here'); break;
    }
    case 'fact': revealFact(); out.push('fact revealed'); break;
    case 'stats': out.push(`level ${level()} · xp ${S.xp} · mined ${S.mined} · achievements ${S.ach.length}/${ACHS.length} · map ${S.pins.length}/${PINS.length}`); break;
    case 'ach':
      if(!S.ach.length) out.push('no advancements yet');
      else ACHS.filter(x=>S.ach.includes(x.id)).forEach(x=>out.push('✔ '+x.n));
      break;
    case 'skin':
      if(a[0]==='reset'){
        S.skin=null; store.set('skin',null);
        if(wikiSkinUrl) setSkin(wikiSkinUrl,'Photo: Wikipedia · check the license');
        else { $('#skinImg').hidden=true; $('#skinFb').hidden=false; $('#credit').textContent='Upload a photo to personalize'; }
        out.push('skin reset');
      }else{ $('#skinIn').click(); out.push('choose an image…'); }
      break;
    case 'sound': toggleSound(); out.push('sound '+(S.sound?'on':'off')); break;
    case 'creeper': {
      const b=document.body;
      b.classList.remove('shake'); void b.offsetWidth; b.classList.add('shake');
      setTimeout(()=>b.classList.remove('shake'),600);
      beep(120,.3,'sawtooth',.08);
      out.push('ssssss… BOOM. (Max would have run.)'); break;
    }
    case 'reset':
      if(a[0]==='yes'){ store.clearAll(); location.reload(); }
      else out.push('this wipes all progress. type: reset yes');
      break;
    default: out.push('unknown command: '+c+' (try help)');
  }
  out.forEach(t=>addLog(t));
}
function openCon(){
  $('#console').classList.add('open');
  if(!$('#conLog').children.length) HELP.forEach(t=>addLog(t));
  $('#conIn').focus();
}
function closeCon(){ $('#console').classList.remove('open'); }
$('#conIn').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  if(e.key==='Enter'){
    const v=e.target.value; e.target.value='';
    conRun(v);
  }
});

/* ================= 18. KEYBOARD ================= */
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  if(e.key==='Escape'){ closeModal(); closeCon(); return; }
  if(/INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT/.test(e.target.tagName)) return;
  if(e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey||e.altKey) return;
  if(e.key==='F3'){ e.preventDefault(); document.body.classList.toggle('f3'); return; }
  const k=e.key.toLowerCase();
  if(k==='t'||k==='/'){ e.preventDefault(); openCon(); return; }
  if(/^[0-9]$/.test(e.key)){
    const id=SEC[(+e.key+9)%10];
    document.getElementById(id).scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'});
    return;
  }
  if(k==='u') toggleUpside();
  else if(k==='r') toggleRain();
  else if(k==='n') cycleTime();
  else if(k==='m') toggleSound();
});

/* ================= 19. SCROLL SPY / PROGRESS ================= */
const secEls=SEC.map(id=>document.getElementById(id));
let scrollQueued=false;
function onScroll(){
  scrollQueued=false;
  const y=scrollY, max=document.documentElement.scrollHeight-innerHeight;
  $('#progress').style.width=(max>0?y/max*100:0)+'%';
  let cur=0;
  secEls.forEach((s,i)=>{ if(s.getBoundingClientRect().top<innerHeight*.4) cur=i; });
  $$('#hotbar .slot').forEach((el,i)=>el.classList.toggle('on',i===cur));
  if(y>innerHeight*.5) unlock('hello');
}
addEventListener('scroll',()=>{
  if(!scrollQueued){ scrollQueued=true; requestAnimationFrame(onScroll); }
},{passive:true});

/* ================= 20. REVEAL / COUNTERS / OBSERVERS ================= */
function countUp(el,target){
  const t0=performance.now(), dur=1200;
  (function step(now){
    const p=clamp((now-t0)/dur,0,1);
    el.textContent=Math.round(target*(1-Math.pow(1-p,3)));
    if(p<1) requestAnimationFrame(step);
  })(t0);
}
const io=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>{
  entries.forEach(en=>{
    if(!en.isIntersecting) return;
    const el=en.target;
    el.classList.add('in');
    if(el.dataset.to!==undefined && el.dataset.to!==''){
      const t=+el.dataset.to;
      if(el.id==='statAge') countUp(el,Math.floor((Date.now()-new Date(2002,3,16))/(365.25*864e5)));
      else if(el.id==='statYrs') countUp(el,new Date().getFullYear()-2012);
      else countUp(el,t);
    }
    if(el.matches('.bar>i')) el.style.width=el.dataset.w+'%';
    if(el.id==='radar' && !radarDone){ radarDone=true; animRadar(); }
    io.unobserve(el);
  });
},{threshold:.25});

const mapIO=new IntersectionObserver(es=>es.forEach(e=>{mapVis=e.isIntersecting;}),{threshold:.1});
mapIO.observe(mapC);
const heroIO=new IntersectionObserver(es=>es.forEach(e=>{heroVisible=e.isIntersecting;}),{threshold:0});
heroIO.observe(cv);

/* ================= 21. MAIN LOOP ================= */
function loop(){
  if(heroVisible) drawWorld();
  if(mapVis) mapDraw();
  fxStep();
  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
}

/* ================= 22. BOOT ================= */
function boot(){
  renderBook(0);
  renderTree();
  renderChest();
  renderFrames();
  renderAch();
  renderInv();
  renderHUD();
  renderCraft();
  renderRecipes();
  updateLoreBar();
  $('#mapCount').textContent=S.pins.length;
  $('#btnSound').textContent=S.sound?'🔊':'🔇';
  if(S.frames.some(Boolean)) {} // frames restored via renderFrames
  if(S.skin) setSkin(S.skin,'Your photo · saved in this browser');
  drawRadar(0);
  updateTime();
  $('#clock').textContent=clockText();
  $$('.reveal, [data-to], .bar>i, #radar').forEach(el=>io.observe(el));
  resizeWorld();
  onScroll();
}

let rzT;
addEventListener('resize',()=>{clearTimeout(rzT);rzT=setTimeout(resizeWorld,150);});

boot();
requestAnimationFrame(loop);

let lp=0;
const msgs=['Generating Brenham, Texas…','Mining Broadway blocks…','Opening the Upside Down gate…','Crafting Max Mayfield…','Loading skateboards…'];
const li=setInterval(()=>{
  lp=Math.min(100,lp+rnd(4,10));
  $('#ldBar').style.width=lp+'%';
  $('#ldTxt').textContent=msgs[Math.min(msgs.length-1,Math.floor(lp/21))];
  if(lp>=100){ clearInterval(li); setTimeout(hideLoader,350); }
},110);

function hideLoader(){
  $('#loader').classList.add('done');
  typer();
  loadWiki();
  setTimeout(()=>toast('🎮','WORLD LOADED','Click the ground to walk and mine. Press / for commands.'),800);
}

## Before you publish

- **Contact email**: replace `hello@example.com` in `CONFIG` with your real address.
- **Verify these claims**: the Tony nomination category, the Critics' Choice nomination, the early TV credits, and the *O'Dessa* details.
- **Photos**: the Wikipedia image has its own license. Upload your own photo if you need full rights.
- **Spider-Man**: the release date is widely reported, but her role has not been officially confirmed.
- **Console**: type `help` to see every command.

// GITHUB - THE RESEARCH ENGINE
let GH={user:null,repos:[]};
async function loadGitHub(){
  $('#ldTxt').textContent='Mining GitHub profile...';
  try{
    const [ur,rr]=await Promise.all([fetch(`https://api.github.com/users/${GH_USER}`),fetch(`https://api.github.com/users/${GH_USER}/repos?per_page=100&sort=updated`)]);
    if(!ur.ok) throw new Error('user '+ur.status);
    const u=await ur.json(); let repos=[]; if(rr.ok) repos=await rr.json();
    GH={user:u,repos}; S.ghUser=u;S.ghRepos=repos;store.set('ghUser',u);store.set('ghRepos',repos);store.set('ghTime',Date.now());
    renderGitHub(); unlock('github');
  }catch(err){
    if(S.ghUser){GH={user:S.ghUser,repos:S.ghRepos||[]};renderGitHub(true);}
    else{$('#wikiBlurb').textContent='Offline: GitHub API blocked. Connect internet and reload for live data. Showing flagship Minecraft_UI placeholder.';renderChestFallback();renderFramesFallback()}
  }
}
function renderGitHub(cached=false){
  const u=GH.user,R=GH.repos||[];
  const name=u.name||u.login, bio=u.bio||'Builder crafting web UIs. Creator of Minecraft_UI.';
  setAvatar(u.avatar_url+`&s=400`,'GitHub avatar · @'+u.login+(cached?' · cached':' · live'));
  $('#skinName').textContent='@'+u.login;
  $('#heroBio').innerHTML=`${esc(bio)}<br><b>${u.followers}</b> followers · <b>${u.public_repos}</b> repos · <b>${u.following}</b> following`;
  $('#wikiBlurb').textContent=`${name} (@${u.login}) — ${bio} Account since ${new Date(u.created_at).getFullYear()}. ${u.location?'Based in '+u.location+'. ':''}${u.blog?'Blog: '+u.blog:''} ${cached?'(cached)':'(live from GitHub API)'}`;
  const stars=R.reduce((a,r)=>a+(r.stargazers_count||0),0);
  const years=Math.max(1,new Date().getFullYear()-new Date(u.created_at).getFullYear());
  animNum($('#statFollowers'),u.followers);animNum($('#statRepos'),u.public_repos);animNum($('#statStars'),stars);animNum($('#statYears'),years);
  $('#mailMeta').textContent=`📍 ${u.location||'Location: see GitHub'} · 👥 ${u.followers} followers · 📦 ${u.public_repos} repos`;
  // trophy
  const feat=R.find(r=>/minecraft/i.test(r.name))||R.slice().sort((a,b)=>b.stargazers_count-a.stargazers_count)[0];
  $('#recog').innerHTML=`
    <div class="panel"><h4>👥 ${u.followers} FOLLOWERS · ${u.following} FOLLOWING</h4><p>${esc(name)} · ${R.length} repos loaded live. Joined ${new Date(u.created_at).toDateString()}.</p><span class="tag ok">✔ LIVE API</span></div>
    <div class="panel"><h4>⭐ ${stars} TOTAL STARS · 🍴 ${R.reduce((a,r)=>a+(r.forks_count||0),0)} FORKS</h4><p>Top repo: <b>${feat?esc(feat.name):'—'}</b> (${feat?feat.stargazers_count:0} ⭐). Proof of shipping.</p><span class="tag ok">✔ COMPUTED</span></div>
    <div class="panel"><h4>⛏️ MINECRAFT_UI · FLAGSHIP</h4><p>${feat?esc(feat.description||'Minecraft-style website UI kit.'):''} Open it live on GitHub.</p><a class="btn sm" href="https://github.com/${GH_USER}/Minecraft_UI" target="_blank" rel="noopener">OPEN REPO</a></div>`;
  renderChest();renderFrames();renderLangs();updateBookMinecraft();
}
function animNum(el,to){let s=0;const t0=performance.now();(function st(n){const p=clamp((n-t0)/1000,0,1);el.textContent=Math.round(to*(1-Math.pow(1-p,3)));if(p<1)requestAnimationFrame(st)})(t0)}
function langIcon(l){if(!l)return '📦';l=l.toLowerCase();if(l.includes('html'))return '🌐';if(l.includes('css'))return '🎨';if(l.includes('javascript')||l.includes('typescript'))return '⚡';if(l.includes('python'))return '🐍';if(l.includes('java')&&!l.includes('script'))return '☕';if(l.includes('c'))return '⚙️';return '📦'}

let cF='all',cQ='',cS='updated';
function repoRarity(r){if(/minecraft/i.test(r.name))return 'mythic';if(r.stargazers_count>=10)return 'legendary';if(r.stargazers_count>=5)return 'epic';if(r.fork)return 'common';return r.language?'rare':'common'}
const RC={common:'#cfcfcf',rare:'#55ffff',epic:'#d47bff',legendary:'#ffaa00',mythic:'#ff5555'};
function filteredRepos(){let R=[...(GH.repos||[])];if(cF==='featured')R=R.filter(r=>/minecraft/i.test(r.name)||r.stargazers_count>0);if(cQ)R=R.filter(r=>`${r.name} ${r.description||''} ${r.language||''}`.toLowerCase().includes(cQ));R.sort((a,b)=>cS==='stars'?b.stargazers_count-a.stargazers_count:cS==='name'?a.name.localeCompare(b.name):new Date(b.updated_at)-new Date(a.updated_at));return R}
function renderChest(){const list=filteredRepos();$('#chest-grid').innerHTML=list.length?list.map(r=>{const rar=repoRarity(r);return `<button class="item" data-id="${r.id}" style="--rc:${RC[rar]}"><span class="rar">${rar}</span><span class="big">${/minecraft/i.test(r.name)?'⛏️':langIcon(r.language)}</span><h5>${esc(r.name)}</h5><span class="meta">⭐ ${r.stargazers_count} · ${esc(r.language||'Mixed')} </span><span class="meta" style="font-size:17px;color:#ddd">${esc((r.description||'No description').slice(0,70))}</span></button>`}).join(''):'<p class="px" style="color:#ccc">EMPTY CHEST · NO REPOS MATCH</p>';$('#chestCount').textContent=`${S.chest.length} opened · ${list.length} shown · ${GH.repos.length} total (live)`}

function renderLangs(){const m={};(GH.repos||[]).forEach(r=>{if(r.language)m[r.language]=(m[r.language]||0)+1});const tot=Object.values(m).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)||1;const top=Object.entries(m).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).slice(0,5);$('#langBars').innerHTML=top.length?top.map(([k,v])=>{const p=Math.round(v/tot*100);return `<div class="skill"><div class="row"><span>${esc(k)}</span><span>${v} repos · ${p}%</span></div><div class="bar"><i data-w="${p}" style="display:block"></i></div></div>`}).join(''):'<p class="mini">No language data.</p>';requestAnimationFrame(()=>$$('#langBars .bar i').forEach(el=>el.style.width=el.dataset.w+'%'))}

function drawRadar(k){const Wd=480,Hd=440,cx=240,cy=220,R=150,N=SKILLS.length;const pt=(i,r)=>{const a=-Math.PI/2+i*2*Math.PI/N;return [cx+Math.cos(a)*r,cy+Math.sin(a)*r]};rg.clearRect(0,0,Wd,Hd);rg.strokeStyle='#444';for(let ring=1;ring<=4;ring++){rg.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<N;i++){const[x,y]=pt(i,R*ring/4);i?rg.lineTo(x,y):rg.moveTo(x,y)}rg.closePath();rg.stroke()}rg.font='22px VT323,monospace';rg.fillStyle='#fff';rg.textAlign='center';for(let i=0;i<N;i++){const[x,y]=pt(i,R);rg.beginPath();rg.moveTo(cx,cy);rg.lineTo(x,y);rg.stroke();const[lx,ly]=pt(i,R+28);rg.fillText(SKILLS[i][2],lx,ly)}rg.beginPath();SKILLS.forEach((s,i)=>{const[x,y]=pt(i,R*s[1]/100*k);i?rg.lineTo(x,y):rg.moveTo(x,y)});rg.closePath();rg.fillStyle='rgba(124,252,0,.3)';rg.fill();rg.strokeStyle='#7CFC00';rg.lineWidth=4;rg.stroke()}

const mapIO=new IntersectionObserver(es=>es.forEach(e=>mapVis=e.isIntersecting),{threshold:.1});mapIO.observe(mapC);
const heroIO=new IntersectionObserver(es=>es.forEach(e=>heroVisible=e.isIntersecting),{threshold:0});heroIO.observe(cv);
function loop(){if(heroVisible)drawWorld();if(mapVis)mapDraw();fxStep();requestAnimationFrame(loop)}
let rzT;addEventListener('resize',()=>{clearTimeout(rzT);rzT=setTimeout(resizeWorld,150)});
// BOOT
function boot(){renderBook(0);renderTree();renderAch();renderInv();renderHUD();renderCraft();renderRecipes();drawRadar(0);updateTime();$('#btnSound').textContent=S.sound?'🔊':'🔇';$$('.reveal,.bar i,#radar').forEach(el=>io.observe(el));resizeWorld();onScroll();if(S.ghUser){GH={user:S.ghUser,repos:S.ghRepos||[]};renderGitHub(true)}}
boot();requestAnimationFrame(loop);
let lp=0;const msgs=['Connecting to GitHub API...','Mining Minecraft_UI blocks...','Building realm map...','Crafting ender chest...','Loading...'];
const li=setInterval(()=>{lp=Math.min(100,lp+rnd(4,10));$('#ldBar').style.width=lp+'%';$('#ldTxt').textContent=msgs[Math.min(4,Math.floor(lp/21))];if(lp>=100){clearInterval(li);setTimeout(()=>{$('#loader').classList.add('done');typer();loadGitHub();setTimeout(()=>toast('🎮','WORLD LOADED','Live GitHub data loading. Press / for console.'),800)},350)}},110);
</script>
</body>
</html>

### To make it 100% yours
1. Change `CONFIG.email` at top of script to your real email to receive the mailbox form.
2. Edit `CHAPTERS` text in JS to add your city, college, real story - right now it's generic + live API so it never lies.
3. Pin `Minecraft_UI` repo description on GitHub - it auto-shows here.
4. Want resume PDF button, custom domain, or Bedrock-dark theme? Say the word and I'll add it to this same file.








its amazing i want 10000x times better. now deeply research about design,animations,intractives, and more then start code







I can't open GitHub or your repo from here, so I haven't read your `Minecraft_UI` source. The page reads its public metadata live from the GitHub API and never fills in numbers it can't verify.

## Research summary

**Design:** Minecraft's GUI language is a 16px grid, beveled gray panels, dark purple tooltips, framed advancement toasts, and a pixel display font. The tiles use noise-based texture variants so the world doesn't look repetitive.

**Motion:** `steps()` easing gives a pixel-stepped feel, and `cubic-bezier` handles UI transitions. Block breaking uses ten crack stages, and the splash text pulses like the title screen.

**Interactivity:** Hold-to-mine, drag-to-place, and a command console with Tab completion and history. The skill tree pans and zooms, and the map has a boat moving along the route.

**Data:** Stats come from `/users/{user}` and `/users/{user}/repos`, with a 10-minute cache to stay under the 60 requests/hour unauthenticated limit. Achievements unlock only when real data meets their condition.

**Performance and accessibility:** The world renders to a low-resolution canvas and pauses when offscreen. There's reduced-motion support, keyboard navigation, and ARIA labels.

## What's new

- **Playable hero world:** walk with A/D or click, hold to mine, right-click to place blocks, and switch blocks with `[` `]` or the hotbar.
- **Real day/night:** follows your device's local clock, with a settings override.
- **Live GitHub data:** profile, repos, languages, a build timeline, and a radar chart derived from your stats.
- **Repo chest:** filters, language chips, search, sort, and a README reader.
- **Computed advancements:** unlocked from real data, such as first repo, 10 stars, Minecraft_UI shipped, and 3+ languages.
- **Skill tree, realm map, crafting table, inventory, and console** with autocomplete.
- **Share card:** exports a PNG profile card.
- **Hidden extras:** a Konami code triggers a creeper blast, and there's an Upside Down mode.

<script>
/* ===================== 1. HELPERS ===================== */
const $=(s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);
const $$=(s,r=document)=>[...r.querySelectorAll(s)];
const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
const lerp=(a,b,t)=>a+(b-a)*t;
const rnd=(a,b)=>a+Math.random()*(b-a);
const sleep=ms=>new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,ms));
const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const hash=(x,y)=>{let n=Math.imul(x|0,374761393)^Math.imul(y|0,668265263);n=Math.imul(n^(n>>>13),1274126177);return((n^(n>>>16))>>>0)/4294967296;};
const mix=(a,b,t)=>a.map((v,i)=>v+(b[i]-v)*t);
const rgb=c=>`rgb(${c[0]|0},${c[1]|0},${c[2]|0})`;
const fmtDate=d=>d?new Date(d).toLocaleDateString(undefined,{year:'numeric',month:'short',day:'numeric'}):'—';
const monthKey=d=>`${d.getFullYear()}-${d.getMonth()+1}`;
const RM=matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
const GH_USER='JENSLINJEMZ', API='https://api.github.com';
const CONFIG={email:'hello@example.com'}; // <- replace with the address that should receive mail
const SEC=['home','lore','map','quest','chest','stats','frames','trophy','craft','mail'];
const SECS=SEC.map(id=>document.getElementById(id));

/* ===================== 2. STORAGE & STATE ===================== */
const store={
  get(k,d){try{const v=localStorage.getItem('jj3_'+k);return v==null?d:JSON.parse(v)}catch(e){return d}},
  set(k,v){try{localStorage.setItem('jj3_'+k,JSON.stringify(v));return true}catch(e){return false}},
  wipe(){Object.keys(localStorage).filter(k=>k.startsWith('jj3_')).forEach(k=>localStorage.removeItem(k))}
};
const S={
  xp:store.get('xp',0), ach:store.get('ach',[]), inv:store.get('inv',{2:16,7:8}),
  mined:store.get('mined',0), placed:store.get('placed',0), chest:store.get('chest',[]),
  chapters:store.get('chapters',[]), pins:store.get('pins',[]), crafts:store.get('crafts',[]),
  cfg:Object.assign({sound:true,fx:true,crt:false,time:'real'},store.get('cfg',{})),
  sel:2, rain:false, upside:false, gh:null, m:null, avURL:null
};
const EMPTY_M={repos:'—',followers:'—',following:'—',stars:'—',forks:'—',langs:'—',langsMap:{},recent30:0,years:'—',created:null,mc:null,months:0};

/* ===================== 3. AUDIO ===================== */
let AC=null, NB=null;
function ac(){
  if(!AC){try{AC=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)()}catch(e){return null}}
  if(AC.state==='suspended') AC.resume();
  return AC;
}
function tone(f,d=.08,type='square',v=.05,delay=0){
  if(!S.cfg.sound) return; const a=ac(); if(!a) return;
  const t=a.currentTime+delay, o=a.createOscillator(), g=a.createGain();
  o.type=type; o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f,t);
  g.gain.setValueAtTime(v,t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+d);
  o.connect(g); g.connect(a.destination); o.start(t); o.stop(t+d+.03);
}
function noise(d=.12,v=.12,f=900){
  if(!S.cfg.sound) return; const a=ac(); if(!a) return;
  if(!NB){NB=a.createBuffer(1,a.sampleRate*.5,a.sampleRate);const c=NB.getChannelData(0);for(let i=0;i<c.length;i++)c[i]=Math.random()*2-1;}
  const s=a.createBufferSource(); s.buffer=NB;
  const fl=a.createBiquadFilter(); fl.type='bandpass'; fl.frequency.value=f;
  const g=a.createGain(); g.gain.setValueAtTime(v,a.currentTime); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,a.currentTime+d);
  s.connect(fl); fl.connect(g); g.connect(a.destination); s.start(); s.stop(a.currentTime+d+.02);
}
const SFX={
  click:()=>tone(440,.04),
  pop:()=>{tone(600,.06);tone(900,.08,'square',.05,.06)},
  toast:()=>tone(988,.1,'triangle',.05),
  level:()=>[523,659,784,1046].forEach((f,i)=>tone(f,.15,'square',.05,i*.1)),
  mine:()=>noise(.06,.1,1400),
  brk:()=>{noise(.16,.18,700);tone(120,.12,'sawtooth',.05)},
  place:()=>{tone(260,.05,'square',.06);noise(.05,.05,2200)},
  low:()=>tone(90,.4,'sawtooth',.06),
  boom:()=>{noise(.6,.3,300);tone(60,.5,'sawtooth',.1)}
};

/* ===================== 4. TOASTS, XP, ACHIEVEMENTS ===================== */
function toast(ic,ti,ms,sfx=true){
  const box=$('#toasts'), d=document.createElement('div');
  d.className='toast';
  d.innerHTML=`<div class="ti">${ic}</div><div><b>${esc(ti)}</b><span>${esc(ms)}</span></div>`;
  box.appendChild(d); if(sfx) SFX.toast();
  while(box.children.length>4) box.firstElementChild.remove();
  setTimeout(()=>{d.classList.add('out');setTimeout(()=>d.remove(),420)},3800);
}
const lvlOf=xp=>Math.floor(xp/200)+1;
function renderXP(){
  $('#lvl').textContent=lvlOf(S.xp);
  const into=S.xp%200;
  $('#xpt').textContent=`${into}/200 XP`;
  $('#xpb').style.width=(into/2)+'%';
}
function addXP(n){
  const before=lvlOf(S.xp); S.xp+=n; store.set('xp',S.xp); renderXP();
  if(lvlOf(S.xp)>before){SFX.level();toast('⭐','LEVEL UP!',`You reached level ${lvlOf(S.xp)}.`,false);}
}
const ACH=[
  {id:'hello',i:'🏠',n:'Hello, World',d:'Scroll into the build.'},

  {id:'r5',i:'🏗️',n:'Building Crew',d:'Have 5 public repos.',gh:m=>m.repos>=5},
  {id:'r10',i:'🏰',n:'Architect',d:'Have 10 public repos.',gh:m=>m.repos>=10},

  {id:'s10',i:'🌟',n:'Star Collector',d:'Earn 10 stars across repos.',gh:m=>m.stars>=10},
  {id:'f10',i:'👥',n:'Party Time',d:'Reach 10 followers.',gh:m=>m.followers>=10},
  {id:'poly',i:'🧪',n:'Polyglot',d:'Use 3 or more languages.',gh:m=>m.langs>=3},

];
function unlock(id){
  if(S.ach.includes(id)) return;
  const a=ACH.find(x=>x.id===id); if(!a) return;
  S.ach.push(id); store.set('ach',S.ach);
  toast(a.i,'ADVANCEMENT MADE!',a.n); addXP(30); renderAch();
}
function checkGH(m){ ACH.forEach(a=>{ if(a.gh && a.gh(m)) unlock(a.id); }); }
function renderAch(){
  $('#achGrid').innerHTML=ACH.map(a=>{
    const got=S.ach.includes(a.id);
    return `<div class="ach-card ${got?'got':''}"><div class="ai">${got?a.i:'❔'}</div><div><h6>${esc(got?a.n:'???')}</h6><p>${esc(a.d)}</p></div></div>`;
  }).join('');
  $('#achCount').textContent=S.ach.length;
  $('#achTotal').textContent=ACH.length;
}

/* ===================== 5. PARTICLES & TOOLTIP ===================== */
const fxc=$('#fxc'), fxg=fxc.getContext('2d'); let FP=[];
const fitFx=()=>{fxc.width=innerWidth;fxc.height=innerHeight}; fitFx(); addEventListener('resize',fitFx);
function burst(x,y,cols,n=12){
  if(!S.cfg.fx) return;
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++) FP.push({x,y,vx:rnd(-3,3),vy:rnd(-7,-2),l:1,c:cols[i%cols.length],s:rnd(4,8)|0});
}
function fxTick(){
  if(!FP.length){ return; }
  fxg.clearRect(0,0,fxc.width,fxc.height);
  FP=FP.filter(p=>p.l>0);
  for(const p of FP){p.vy+=.4;p.x+=p.vx;p.y+=p.vy;p.l-=.025;fxg.globalAlpha=Math.max(0,p.l);fxg.fillStyle=p.c;fxg.fillRect(p.x|0,p.y|0,p.s,p.s)}
  fxg.globalAlpha=1;
}
const tip=$('#tip'); let tipEl=null;
document.addEventListener('pointerover',e=>{
  const t=e.target.closest?.('[data-tip]'); if(!t||t===tipEl) return;
  tipEl=t; const [a,b]=t.dataset.tip.split('|');
  tip.innerHTML=`<b>${esc(a)}</b>${b?`<em>${esc(b)}</em>`:''}`; tip.classList.add('on');
});
document.addEventListener('pointerout',e=>{
  if(tipEl&&!tipEl.contains(e.relatedTarget)){tipEl=null;tip.classList.remove('on')}
});
document.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
  if(!tipEl) return;
  tip.style.left=Math.min(innerWidth-tip.offsetWidth-8,e.clientX+18)+'px';
  tip.style.top=Math.min(innerHeight-tip.offsetHeight-8,e.clientY+18)+'px';
});
document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
  const t=e.target.closest?.('.btn,.stt'); if(t) burst(e.clientX,e.clientY,['#5EBC2E','#79553A','#8a8a8a','#FFEB3B'],8);
});

/* ===================== 6. WORLD ENGINE ===================== */
const TILE=8, TW=220, TH=72, PX=2;
const B={AIR:0,GRASS:1,DIRT:2,STONE:3,ORE:4,WOOD:5,LEAF:6,PLANK:7,BED:8};
const BN={1:'Grass',2:'Dirt',3:'Stone',4:'Star Ore',5:'Wood',6:'Leaves',7:'Planks'};
const BE={1:'🟩',2:'🟫',3:'⬜',4:'⭐',5:'🪵',6:'🍃',7:'🟧'};
const HARD={1:.5,2:.45,3:1.1,4:1.6,5:.9,6:.2,7:.8,8:Infinity};
const PLACE=[2,1,3,5,7,4,6];
const PAL={1:['#5EBC2E','#4fae22','#6ccc38'],2:['#79553A','#6b4a31','#8a6244'],3:['#7a7a7a','#8a8a8a','#6a6a6a'],4:['#7a7a7a','#d9b44a','#fff1a0'],5:['#5b3b1d','#6a4422','#4a2e14'],6:['#2f7a1c','#3f9a2a','#2a6a18'],7:['#A9824B','#9a7440','#b98f57'],8:['#222','#333','#111']};
const PAL_UP={1:['#8a1c1c','#7a1818','#9a2222'],2:['#4a1a1a','#3d1414','#5a2020'],3:['#5a2c2c','#6a3434','#4a2222'],4:['#5a2c2c','#ff5a3a','#ffb08a'],5:['#2a0b0b','#3a1010','#1f0606'],6:['#4a0c0c','#5e1414','#3a0808'],7:['#6a3a2a','#5a3020','#7a4a36'],8:['#110','#200','#080']};
function buildAtlas(Pl){
  const c=document.createElement('canvas'); c.width=TILE*36; c.height=TILE; const a=c.getContext('2d');
  for(let id=1;id<=8;id++) for(let v=0;v<4;v++){
    const ox=(id*4+v)*TILE, pal=Pl[id];
    for(let py=0;py<TILE;py++) for(let px=0;px<TILE;px++){
      const h=hash(px*3+v*31+id*7,py*5+v*17+id*11); let col;
      if(id===1) col=py<2?pal[(h*3)|0]:Pl[2][(h*2)|0];
      else if(id===4) col=h>.92?pal[1]:h>.84?pal[2]:Pl[3][(h*3)|0];
      else if(id===6) col=h<.14?null:pal[(h*3)|0];
      else if(id===5) col=px%4===0?pal[2]:pal[(h*2)|0];
      else if(id===7) col=py%4===3?pal[1]:pal[(h*2)|0];
      else col=pal[(h*pal.length)|0];
      if(col){a.fillStyle=col;a.fillRect(ox+px,py,1,1)}
    }
    if(id!==6){a.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.22)';a.fillRect(ox+TILE-1,0,1,TILE);a.fillRect(ox,TILE-1,TILE,1)}
  }
  return c;
}
let ATLAS=null, ATL_CUR=null;
const TC=document.createElement('canvas'); TC.width=TW*TILE; TC.height=TH*TILE;
const tc=TC.getContext('2d');
let grid=new Uint8Array(TW*TH), colTop=new Int16Array(TW);
const gi=(x,y)=>y*TW+x;
const getT=(x,y)=>(x<0||y<0||x>=TW||y>=TH)?0:grid[y*TW+x];
const solid=(x,y)=>getT(x,y)!==0;
function paintT(x,y){
  const id=grid[gi(x,y)], dx=x*TILE, dy=y*TILE;
  tc.clearRect(dx,dy,TILE,TILE);
  if(!id) return;
  const v=(hash(x,y)*4)|0;
  tc.drawImage(ATL_CUR,(id*4+v)*TILE,0,TILE,TILE,dx,dy,TILE,TILE);
}
function topOf(x){ for(let y=0;y<TH;y++) if(grid[gi(x,y)]) return y; return TH; }
function setT(x,y,id){
  if(x<0||y<0||x>=TW||y>=TH) return;
  grid[gi(x,y)]=id; paintT(x,y);
  if(!id){ if(y===colTop[x]) colTop[x]=topOf(x); }
  else if(y<colTop[x]) colTop[x]=y;
}
function genWorld(){
  grid.fill(0); const s=rnd(0,50);
  for(let x=0;x<TW;x++){
    const h=Math.round(34+Math.sin(x*.045+s)*3+Math.sin(x*.13+s*1.7)*1.5+Math.sin(x*.31+s)*.6);
    for(let y=h;y<TH;y++){
      let id=y===h?B.GRASS:(y<h+4?B.DIRT:B.STONE);
      if(y===TH-1) id=B.BED;
      else if(id===B.STONE && hash(x+500,y)>.965) id=B.ORE;
      grid[gi(
